# pixi-silk: text, layout, UI widgets and the editor kit

> Smooth vector graphics for PixiJS v8. Every shape is an exact signed distance field, anti-aliased per pixel in the shader, so edges stay smooth at any zoom, rotation and device pixel ratio without MSAA.

pixi-silk 1.0.0 is an MIT-licensed TypeScript library. It needs pixi.js ^8.6 as a peer dependency, and a browser with WebGL2 or WebGPU.

Site: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev
Repository: https://github.com/schmooky/pixi-silk
Package: https://www.npmjs.com/package/pixi-silk

This file holds the guides on text, layout, UI widgets and the editor kit, with the source of their live demos, then the API of their entry points. Each lives under its own import path.

- [llms-full.txt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/llms-full.txt): core, paint and rendering: guides and API, SilkGraphics first.
- [llms-examples.txt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/llms-examples.txt): whole screens with the source of every demo.

## Gotchas

- WebGL2 is the default renderer. For WebGPU pass `createSilkApp({ preference: 'webgpu' })`, or in your own Pixi app call `enableWebGPU()` from `pixi-silk/webgpu` before you create the first SilkGraphics.
- Stroke joins are `miter` by default, as in Canvas 2D and Pixi. `bevel` and `round` are the others. `miterLimit` defaults to 10.
- Free-form paths (`moveTo`, `lineTo`, Bezier curves, `poly`, `svgPath`) both fill and stroke. A fill paints a path as one exact even-odd shape.
- Shape operations: `fill({ op: 'union' | 'subtract' | 'intersect' | 'exclude' })` on the shapes of the active path, `g.boolean(op, a, b)` to nest them, and `g.svgPath(d)` for SVG path data.
- Batching is off by default: each SilkGraphics is one draw call. Call `enableSilkBatching()` before you create the objects to batch runs of them, on WebGL2 and WebGPU. `{ batch: false }` keeps one object out.
- Each primitive is one instance of 44 floats (11 vec4, 176 bytes).
- The root import holds the core only. UI widgets, editor tools, text, layout, glass, glow and WebGPU are separate entry points such as `pixi-silk/ui/button` or `pixi-silk/text`.
- A SmartContainer does nothing until it has a `viewport` (the Application, its renderer, a signal or a rectangle).
- `clear()` keeps the GPU buffers, so clearing and redrawing a SilkGraphics every frame is cheap.
- There are no texture fills and no `svg()` import. Use gradients, and `svgPath(d)` for path data.

## Entry points

Import each from its own path. The root import does not re-export the others.

| Import | Area | What it holds |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [`pixi-silk`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api.md) | Core | SilkGraphics, createSilkApp, gradients (linear, radial, conic, along, mesh), colour and curve helpers, motion helpers, enableSilkBatching. |
| [`pixi-silk/glass`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/glass.md) | Paint | Glass: a frosted pane that blurs and bends what is behind it. |
| [`pixi-silk/glow`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/glow.md) | Paint | Glow, Aurora and light helpers (neon, halo, rays, spectrum). |
| [`pixi-silk/webgpu`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/webgpu.md) | Rendering | enableWebGPU: installs the WGSL shader for your own WebGPU Application. |
| [`pixi-silk/text`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text.md) | Text | SilkText: distance-field text with outline, shadow and gradient fill. SdfAtlas, layoutText, caret helpers. |
| [`pixi-silk/layout`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout.md) | Layout | LayoutNode: a headless flex layout engine with no Pixi in it. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/layout`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout.md) | Layout | SmartContainer and FlexContainer: place and scale a design box in the window, flex children inside it. |
| [`pixi-silk/signal`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal.md) | UI widgets | signal, computed, watch, batch: the small reactive store every widget state uses. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/radial`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/radial.md) | UI widgets | RadialMenu and bindRadialTrigger: a ring menu with submenus. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/button`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/button.md) | UI widgets | SilkButton: pill, circle or squircle button with press, hold, busy and appear animations. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/dock`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dock.md) | UI widgets | ButtonDock: a row or column of SilkButtons that closes the gap when one hides. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/halo`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo.md) | UI widgets | ContextHalo: a ring of actions around a selected object. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/modes`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/modes.md) | UI widgets | SegmentedSwitch and ModeSwitcher: tool mode pickers, including the hold-a-key ring. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/dial`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial.md) | UI widgets | JogDial and Scrubber: rotary and drag-to-change number inputs. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/odometer`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer.md) | UI widgets | Odometer: a rolling number counter with prefix, decimals and count-up timing. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/gesture`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture.md) | UI widgets | trackGesture: tap, hold, flick and drag from one target, with slop per pointer kind. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/arc`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc.md) | UI widgets | Angle maths for ring widgets: angleDelta, wrapAngle, snapTo, rubberBand. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/keys`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys.md) | UI widgets | Shared keyboard state: isKeyDown, modifier checks, onKey, isTypingTarget. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/pill`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pill.md) | UI widgets | ValuePill: a small rounded label for live readouts (angles, sizes, distances). |
| [`pixi-silk/ui`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui.md) | UI widgets | Every widget and editor tool in one import. The per-widget subpaths ship less code. |
| [`pixi-silk/selection`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/selection.md) | Editor kit | Selection store and bindOutline, bindGizmo, bindMeasure. |
| [`pixi-silk/history`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history.md) | Editor kit | History (undo and redo), recordSignals, recordGizmo, bindHistoryKeys. |
| [`pixi-silk/path`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path.md) | Editor kit | Bezier path model: BezierPath, drawPath, parseSvgPath, pathToSvg and edit operations. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/gizmo`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gizmo.md) | Editor kit | TransformGizmo: move, rotate, scale and box handles. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/outline`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/outline.md) | Editor kit | SelectionOutline: hover and selection outlines. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/pathgizmo`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo.md) | Editor kit | PathGizmo and PenTool: edit and draw Bezier paths. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/camera`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera.md) | Editor kit | Camera: pan and zoom a world container, ZoomControl. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/measure`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure.md) | Editor kit | Measure: size labels and red lines between objects. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/grid`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid.md) | Editor kit | CanvasGrid: dots or lines that follow the camera. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/ruler`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler.md) | Editor kit | Ruler: screen-edge rulers that follow the camera. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/guides`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides.md) | Editor kit | Guides: draggable guide lines from the rulers. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/marquee`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee.md) | Editor kit | Marquee: drag a box to select. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/group`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/group.md) | Editor kit | SelectionGroup: transform several objects as one. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/spacing`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing.md) | Editor kit | SpacingMarkers and findSpacing: equal-gap hints. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/snap`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap.md) | Editor kit | SnapEngine and SnapGuides: snap boxes to edges, centres and lines. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/color`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color.md) | Editor kit | Colour pickers: wheels, rings, OKLCH, channel sliders. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/brush`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/brush.md) | Editor kit | BrushCursor: a brush size and hardness cursor. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/timeline`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline.md) | Editor kit | MiniTimeline: frames, keys, loop range and playback. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/textedit`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/textedit.md) | Editor kit | TextEditor: an editable SilkText field with caret, selection and IME. |

| [`pixi-silk/ui/layers`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers.md) | Editor kit | LayerTree: a layer tree over any data through an adapter, with selection and history. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/inspector`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector.md) | Editor kit | Inspector: property fields bound to the selection. |
| [`pixi-silk/ui/minimap`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/minimap.md) | Editor kit | Minimap: an overview of the world with the camera frame. |

# Your first editor canvas

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/first-editor-canvas/
Area: Learn

> Build an editor canvas in PixiJS v8 with the pixi-silk editor kit: a camera that pans and zooms, a grid, a selection store, outlines and a transform gizmo.

This tutorial builds the core of a design tool canvas: a camera that pans and zooms, a grid,
click to select and a gizmo that moves the selected layer.

> **NOTE**
> Experimental in 1.0: the API may change in a minor release.

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `editor-canvas` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { linear, SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { bindHistoryKeys, History, recordGizmo } from 'pixi-silk/history';
import { bindGizmo, bindMeasure, bindOutline, Selection } from 'pixi-silk/selection';
import { watch } from 'pixi-silk/signal';
import { SilkText, silkLabel } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { Camera, ZoomControl } from 'pixi-silk/ui/camera';
import { TransformGizmo } from 'pixi-silk/ui/gizmo';
import { altDown } from 'pixi-silk/ui/keys';
import { Measure } from 'pixi-silk/ui/measure';
import { SelectionOutline } from 'pixi-silk/ui/outline';
import { defineDemo, INK } from './runtime';

// Scroll to pan, pinch or ctrl + scroll to zoom, right drag or Space + drag to pan.
// Click a layer to select it, Shift + click to add more. Hover another to measure the distance
// to it. Drag the arrows to move a layer, cmd or ctrl + Z undoes and Shift + cmd + Z redoes.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Editor canvas',
    size: [720, 480],
    touchAction: 'none',
    ownsCamera: true,
    controls: {
        redlines: { type: 'select', label: 'redlines', options: ['on hover', 'with Alt'], value: 'on hover' },
    },
    setup({ silk, params }) {
        const app = silk.app;
        const world = new Container();
        const overlay = new Container();
        const camera = new Camera({ world, element: app.canvas });
        const hover = new SelectionOutline({ ticker: app.ticker });
        const outline = new SelectionOutline({ ticker: app.ticker });
        const gizmo = new TransformGizmo({ ticker: app.ticker });
        // SilkText labels: distance fields stay sharp at any zoom
        const label = silkLabel();
        const measure = new Measure({ space: world, ticker: app.ticker, label });
        const zoom = new ZoomControl(camera, { label });
        // one selection store drives the outlines, the gizmo and the redlines
        const selection = new Selection<Container>();
        const history = new History();

        gizmo.state.mode.set('move');
        overlay.addChild(hover, outline, gizmo, measure, zoom);
        app.stage.addChild(world, overlay);
        app.stage.eventMode = 'static';
        app.stage.hitArea = app.screen;
        app.stage.on('pointerdown', (e) => e.target === app.stage && !e.shiftKey && selection.clear());

        const redlines = () => params.redlines === 'on hover' || altDown();
        const measured = bindMeasure(selection, measure, { between: redlines });
        // while measuring, the hovered layer gets the red outline instead of the blue one
        const syncHover = () => {
            const over = selection.state.hovered.value;
            const quiet = !over || selection.has(over) || (redlines() && selection.primary);

            hover.hover(quiet ? null : over);
        };
        const stops = [
            bindOutline(selection, outline),
            watch(selection.state.hovered, syncHover),
            watch(selection.state.items, syncHover),
            bindGizmo(selection, gizmo),
            measured.stop,
            // every gizmo drag is one undo step, and the keys undo it
            recordGizmo(gizmo, history),
            bindHistoryKeys(history),
        ];

        // an artboard with a few layers on it
        const title = new SilkText({
            text: 'Home',
            fontFamily: 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif',
            fontSize: 13,
            fill: INK.muted,
        });

        title.position.set(0, -20);
        world.addChild(title);

        const layer = (x: number, y: number, draw: (g: SilkGraphics) => void) => {
            const item = new Container();
            const g = new SilkGraphics();

            draw(g);
            item.addChild(g);
            item.position.set(x, y);
            item.eventMode = 'static';
            item.cursor = 'pointer';
            item.on('pointerover', () => selection.state.hovered.set(item));
            item.on('pointerout', () => {
                if (selection.state.hovered.value === item) selection.state.hovered.set(null);
            });
            item.on('pointerdown', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                selection.pick(item, e.shiftKey ? 'toggle' : 'replace');
            });
            world.addChild(item);

            return item;
        };

        const board = layer(0, 0, (g) => g.rect(0, 0, 390, 620).fill(0xf4f4f6));

        layer(24, 24, (g) =>
            g.roundRect(0, 0, 342, 180, 24, 0.6).fill(linear([0x5e5ce6, 0xbf5af2], { from: [0, 0], to: [1, 1] })),
        );
        layer(24, 228, (g) =>
            g
                .roundRect(0, 0, 160, 120, 18, 0.6)
                .fill({ color: 0xffffff, innerShadow: { color: 0x000000, alpha: 0.06, y: -1, blur: 1 } }),
        );

        layer(206, 228, (g) => g.roundRect(0, 0, 160, 120, 18, 0.6).fill(0xffffff));
        layer(24, 372, (g) => g.roundRect(0, 0, 342, 64, 16, 0.6).fill(0xffffff));
        layer(48, 388, (g) => g.circle(16, 16, 16).fill(0x30d158));
        layer(24, 532, (g) => g.pill(0, 0, 342, 52).fill(0x1c1c1e));

        const place = () =>
            zoom.position.set(app.screen.width - zoom.size[0] - 12, app.screen.height - zoom.size[1] - 12);

        app.renderer.on('resize', place);
        place();
        camera.fit(board, 40, false);

        // Alt shows the redlines without moving the pointer
        const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key !== 'Alt') return;
            measured.sync();
            syncHover();
        };

        addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        addEventListener('keyup', onKey);

        return () => {
            for (const stop of stops) stop();
            removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
            removeEventListener('keyup', onKey);
            camera.destroy();
        };
    },
});
```

The demo adds redlines and undo to what this page builds. Its source opens in Studio.

### The whole canvas

```ts

// 1. a world the camera moves, and an overlay that stays in screen space
const world = new Container();
const overlay = new Container();
const camera = new Camera({ world, element: app.canvas });
const grid = new CanvasGrid({ camera, ticker: app.ticker });

app.stage.addChild(grid, world, overlay);

// 2. one selection store, with an outline and a gizmo that follow it
const selection = new Selection<Container>();
const outline = new SelectionOutline({ ticker: app.ticker });
const gizmo = new TransformGizmo({ mode: 'move', ticker: app.ticker });

overlay.addChild(outline, gizmo);
bindOutline(selection, outline);
bindGizmo(selection, gizmo);

// 3. layers that select on click
function layer(x: number, y: number, draw: (g: SilkGraphics) => void) {
    const g = new SilkGraphics();

    draw(g);
    g.position.set(x, y);
    g.eventMode = 'static';
    g.cursor = 'pointer';
    g.on('pointerdown', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        selection.pick(g, e.shiftKey ? 'toggle' : 'replace');
    });
    world.addChild(g);

    return g;
}

const card = layer(0, 0, (g) => g.roundRect(0, 0, 200, 120, 18, 0.6).fill(0xffffff));

layer(240, 20, (g) => g.circle(40, 40, 40).fill(0x30d158));

// 4. a click on empty canvas clears the selection
app.stage.eventMode = 'static';
app.stage.hitArea = app.screen;
app.stage.on('pointerdown', (e) => e.target === app.stage && selection.clear());

selection.pick(card);
camera.fit(world, 40, false);
```

### What each part does

The camera moves and scales `world`. Scroll pans it, and pinch or ctrl + scroll zooms around the
pointer. `camera.fit(world, 40, false)` frames the layers with 40 px of padding and no animation.

The grid sits behind the world and reads the camera, so its lines stay `lineWidth` screen pixels
wide (default 1) at every zoom. Finer levels fade in as you zoom in.

`Selection` is a store with no drawing in it. `bindOutline` and `bindGizmo` subscribe the outline
and the gizmo to it, and each returns a function that stops the binding. Everything that changes
the selection goes through `selection.pick()` or `selection.clear()`.

The outline and the gizmo live in `overlay`, outside the world. They follow their target's
screen position, so the handles keep their size when you zoom. Put overlays inside the world and
they scale with it.

### Options worth changing

| Option | Default | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `Camera` `wheel` | `'pan'` | `'zoom'` makes the wheel zoom, like a map |
| `Camera` `zoomRange` | `[0.02, 256]` | smallest and largest zoom |
| `Camera` `touchPan` | `'two'` | `'one'` pans with one finger |
| `CanvasGrid` `style` | `'lines'` | `'dots'` draws a dot grid |
| `TransformGizmo` `mode` | `'universal'` | `'move'`, `'rotate'`, `'scale'` or `'box'` shows one kind of handle |

### Where it stops

The kit draws and tracks state. It does not save documents or decide what a layer is. The
selection holds any value you give it, so your own layer objects work as well as containers.

### Where next

- [Canvas & rulers](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/canvas-and-rulers/) adds rulers, guides, a marquee and spacing markers.
- [Gizmos & selection](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/gizmos-and-selection/) covers rotate and scale, snapping and undo.
- [Panels](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/panels/) adds a layer tree and an inspector.

---

# Text

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/text/
Area: Text

> SilkText draws labels and readouts from a runtime SDF glyph atlas, with outlines, glows, gradients and tabular numbers. It lives in pixi-silk/text.

`SilkText` from `pixi-silk/text` draws a string from distance-field glyphs, so a label under a
zooming camera keeps its edges where PixiJS `Text` turns into a stretched bitmap.

### Draw a label

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `sdf-text` (source):

```ts
import { Container, Graphics, Text } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { defineDemo, FONT, INK, label, PALETTE, readout } from './runtime';

// Left: PixiJS Text, a bitmap drawn once. Right: SilkText, distance fields drawn per pixel.
// Both zoom around the same point. The animation holds at 1x, zooms to 8x and back.
// Turn it off and drag the slider for up to 100x.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Text and SilkText under zoom',
    size: [720, 320],
    controls: {
        zoom: { type: 'range', label: 'zoom (log)', min: 0, max: 2, step: 0.01, value: 0, unit: '' },
        auto: { type: 'toggle', label: 'animate', value: true },
    },
    setup({ stage, params, tick }) {
        const half = 360;
        const top = 48;
        const sides: Container[] = [];
        const style = { fontFamily: FONT, fontWeight: '600' as const };

        for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
            const side = new Container();
            const mask = new Graphics().rect(i * half, top, half, 320 - top).fill(0xffffff);
            const make = (text: string, size: number, color: number) =>
                i === 0
                    ? new Text({ text, style: { ...style, fontSize: size, fill: color } })
                    : new SilkText({ text, ...style, fontSize: size, fill: color });
            const title = make('Zoom 42%', 22, INK.text);
            const body = make('Labels, readouts and editor text', 13, INK.muted);
            const tiny = make('W 128  H 64', 11, PALETTE.blue);

            title.position.set(-100, -34);
            body.position.set(-100, 0);
            tiny.position.set(-100, 22);
            side.addChild(title, body, tiny);
            side.mask = mask;
            sides.push(side);
            stage.addChild(side, mask);
            stage.addChild(
                label(i ? 'SilkText (distance field)' : 'Text (bitmap)', i * half + 24, 16, { fontSize: 13 }),
            );
        }
        stage.addChild(new Graphics().rect(half - 0.5, 0, 1, 320).fill(INK.line));
        stage.addChild(new Graphics().rect(0, top - 0.5, 720, 1).fill(INK.line));
        const zoom = readout('', 696, 16, { fontSize: 13 }, 1);

        stage.addChild(zoom);

        // the point both sides zoom into (the "42" of the title), and the middle of the text block
        const focus = { x: -26, y: -21 };
        const block = { x: 25, y: 0 };
        const ease = (x: number) => 0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(Math.PI * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, x)));
        let t = 0;

        tick((_t, dt) => {
            if (params.auto) {
                t += dt;
                // a 10 s loop: 3 s at 1x, 2.5 s up to 8x, 2 s there, 2.5 s back
                const u = t % 10;
                const up = u < 3 ? 0 : u < 5.5 ? ease((u - 3) / 2.5) : u < 7.5 ? 1 : 1 - ease((u - 7.5) / 2.5);

                params.zoom = up * Math.log10(8);
            }
            const k = 10 ** params.zoom;

            // at 1x the block sits in the middle of its half, zoomed in the focus point does
            sides.forEach((side, i) => {
                side.pivot.set(focus.x, focus.y);
                side.position.set(
                    i * half + half / 2 - (block.x - focus.x) / k,
                    top + (320 - top) / 2 - (block.y - focus.y) / k,
                );
                side.scale.set(k);
            });
            zoom.text = `${k.toFixed(1)}x`;
        });
    },
});
```

```ts

const label = new SilkText({
    text: 'Frame 1',
    fontFamily: 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif',
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: 600,
    fill: 0xffffff,
    anchor: 0.5,
});

app.stage.addChild(label);
label.text = 'Frame 2'; // laid out again before the next frame
```

PixiJS `Text` draws the string once into a canvas and shows that as a texture. SilkText stores
each glyph as a signed distance field instead, and the shader turns the distance into coverage per
screen pixel. [How it works](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/how-it-works/) covers the idea. All texts share one atlas texture.

Change one field with its setter (`label.fontSize = 18`) or several with `label.set({ ... })`.
Width, height, bounds and hit tests use the text box, as with PixiJS `Text`.

### Add an outline, a glow or a gradient

Live demo `sdf-text-styles` (source):

```ts
import { Text } from 'pixi.js';
import { horizontal, SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { defineDemo, FONT, INK, label, PALETTE } from './runtime';

// Outline, glow, shadow, weight and a gradient all read the same distance field: no filters,
// still one draw call per text. Bottom left: small sizes at 1x, PixiJS Text next to SilkText.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'SilkText styles',
    size: [720, 380],
    controls: {
        outline: { type: 'range', label: 'outline', min: 0, max: 6, step: 0.5, value: 3, unit: 'px' },
        glow: { type: 'range', label: 'glow', min: 0, max: 12, step: 0.5, value: 8, unit: 'px' },
        dilate: { type: 'range', label: 'weight', min: -1.5, max: 1.5, step: 0.1, value: 0, unit: 'px' },
    },
    setup({ stage, params, tick }) {
        const card = new SilkGraphics()
            .roundRect(24, 196, 440, 160, 20, 0.6)
            .fill(INK.surface)
            .roundRect(484, 196, 212, 160, 20, 0.6)
            .fill(INK.surface);
        const outlined = new SilkText({
            text: 'OUTLINE',
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: 44,
            fontWeight: 900,
            fill: PALETTE.yellow,
            outline: { width: 3, color: 0x3a1c00 },
            shadow: { color: 0x000000, alpha: 0.6, blur: 6, offsetY: 4 },
        });
        const neon = new SilkText({
            text: 'Neon glow',
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: 40,
            fontWeight: 700,
            fill: 0xffe3f1,
            shadow: { color: 0xff2d92, blur: 8 },
            anchor: { x: 1, y: 0 },
        });
        const gradient = new SilkText({
            text: 'Gradient fill',
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: 34,
            fontWeight: 800,
            fill: horizontal(['#64d2ff', '#bf5af2', '#ff375f']),
        });
        const value = new SilkText({
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: 44,
            fontWeight: 600,
            fill: INK.text,
            tabularNumbers: true,
            anchor: { x: 1, y: 0 },
        });
        const unit = new SilkText({
            text: 'BPM, live',
            fontFamily: FONT,
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: 600,
            fill: PALETTE.pink,
        });

        outlined.position.set(36, 28);
        neon.position.set(684, 32);
        gradient.position.set(36, 116);
        value.position.set(672, 240);
        unit.position.set(508, 316);
        stage.addChild(card, outlined, neon, gradient, value, unit);
        stage.addChild(label('Tabular numbers', 508, 214));

        // small sizes at 1x: PixiJS Text in the left column, SilkText in the right one
        const sample = 'Hamburgefonstiv 0123';

        stage.addChild(label('Text', 92, 212), label('SilkText', 280, 212));
        [11, 12, 13].forEach((size, i) => {
            const y = 248 + i * 34;
            const pixi = new Text({ text: sample, style: { fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: size, fill: INK.text } });
            const silk = new SilkText({ text: sample, fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: size, fill: INK.text });

            pixi.position.set(92, y);
            silk.position.set(280, y);
            stage.addChild(pixi, silk, label(`${size}px`, 44, y + 1, { fontSize: 11, fill: INK.faint }));
        });

        tick((t) => {
            outlined.outline = params.outline > 0 ? { width: params.outline, color: 0x3a1c00 } : null;
            neon.shadow =
                params.glow > 0 ? { color: 0xff2d92, blur: params.glow * (0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(t * 3)) } : null;
            for (const text of [outlined, neon, gradient]) text.dilate = params.dilate;
            value.text = (72 + 6 * Math.sin(t * 1.3) + 2 * Math.sin(t * 4.1)).toFixed(1);
        });
    },
});
```

An outline or a glow reads the same distance field at a different distance, so it needs no filter.

```ts

const title = new SilkText({
    text: 'BIG WIN',
    fontSize: 48,
    fontWeight: 900,
    fill: horizontal(['#ffd60a', '#ff9f0a']),
    outline: { width: 3, color: 0x3a1c00 },
    shadow: { color: 0x000000, alpha: 0.6, blur: 6, offsetY: 4 },
});
const readout = new SilkText({ text: '72.4', fontSize: 32, tabularNumbers: true });
```

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `fontFamily`, `fontSize`, `fontWeight`, `fontStyle` | system UI font, 16, 400, normal | Any CSS font. Glyphs are baked once at a fixed size |
| `fill` | white | A colour, or a linear, radial or conic gradient over the text box |
| `letterSpacing`, `lineHeight` | 0, font | Spacing in local units. Line height 0 uses the font's own |
| `align`, `wordWrap`, `wordWrapWidth`, `breakWords` | left, off, 100, off | Line layout, as in PixiJS `Text` |
| `anchor` | 0 | Point of the text box at the position |
| `outline` | none | `{ width, color, alpha }`, outside the glyph edge |
| `shadow` | none | `{ color, alpha, blur, offsetX, offsetY }`. Without an offset it is a glow |
| `dilate` | 0 | Grows or thins the glyphs, in local units |
| `tabularNumbers` | off | Digits share one width, so a changing number does not jitter |
| `kerning` | on | Pair kerning measured from Canvas |

Outlines and glows reach about a quarter of the font size. Past that the distance field runs out
and the effect is clamped. For a wider halo, put a blurred SilkGraphics shape behind the text.

### Small sizes

At 11 to 13 px a texel of the atlas is smaller than a screen pixel. SilkText then averages four
samples per pixel, so thin stems do not flicker. The bottom left of the first demo shows both
kinds of text at 11, 12 and 13 px.

Glyph shapes and widths match PixiJS `Text`, because both come from the same Canvas font and
`measureText`. Some platforms hint Canvas text to the pixel grid, so at 1x and no zoom its stems
can look a little crisper. From 2x, or under a camera, SilkText wins.

### Use SilkText inside widgets

Widgets that show a value take a `label` option, a function that builds their text. Pass
`silkLabel()` for widgets that sit under a zooming camera. The widget never imports SilkText
itself, so apps that do not use it do not ship it.

```ts

const pill = new ValuePill({ label: silkLabel({ tabularNumbers: true }) });

pill.show('42 px');
```

To let people click and type into a text, wrap it in a `TextEditor`. See [Editing text](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/editing-text/).

### Fonts and the atlas

Each glyph is drawn with Canvas2D at 48 px the first time it appears, then turned into a distance
field. The atlas is one 8-bit texture, 1024 texels wide, that doubles in height up to 4096. When it
is full it starts over, and every text bakes what it needs again.

Load web fonts before the first text uses them, for example with `document.fonts.load('600 16px Inter')`.
Glyphs baked while a font was still loading are baked again once it is ready.

For a different trade-off, make your own atlas and pass it to the texts that should use it:

```ts

const big = new SdfAtlas({ size: 64, radius: 24 }); // sharper corners far in, longer glows
const heading = new SilkText({ text: 'Overview', fontSize: 40, atlas: big });
```

### Where it stops

Corners round off slightly at extreme zoom, because a single-channel distance field cannot hold a
sharp corner. At the default 48 px bake the rounding is about a fiftieth of the font size.

Emoji and colour fonts come out as one-colour silhouettes. There is no shaping: no ligatures, no
right-to-left text and no combining marks, and each code point is one glyph. Latin, Greek and
Cyrillic UI text works. Use PixiJS `Text` for emoji, Arabic and Indic scripts.

SilkText runs on WebGL2 and WebGPU. Pixi's WebGL1 fallback is not supported.

---

# Editing text

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/editing-text/
Area: Text

> TextEditor turns a SilkText into a text layer people click and type into: caret, selection, undo, clipboard, forms and input methods, on the canvas.

`TextEditor` from `pixi-silk/ui/textedit` wraps a [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/text/) with a caret, a
selection and keyboard input. It is a display object, so it pans and zooms with a
[camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/canvas-and-rulers/) like the rest of the scene.

### Make a text layer editable

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `text-edit-canvas` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { linear, SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { bindHistoryKeys, History } from 'pixi-silk/history';
import { SilkText, silkLabel } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { Camera, ZoomControl } from 'pixi-silk/ui/camera';
import { TextEditor } from 'pixi-silk/ui/textedit';
import { defineDemo } from './runtime';

// Click a text to edit it, drag to select, double click a word. Pinch or ctrl + scroll to zoom in
// and keep typing: the caret, the selection and the glyphs stay sharp. Scroll pans.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Text editing on the canvas',
    size: [760, 440],
    touchAction: 'none',
    ownsCamera: true,
    setup({ silk }) {
        const app = silk.app;
        const world = new Container();
        const camera = new Camera({ world, element: app.canvas });
        const zoom = new ZoomControl(camera, { label: silkLabel() });
        // one undo stack for the whole canvas: text edits sit in it with everything else
        const history = new History();
        const font = 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif';

        app.stage.addChild(world, zoom);

        const card = new SilkGraphics()
            .roundRect(0, 0, 420, 260, 28, 0.6)
            .fill(linear([0x2c2c30, 0x1c1c1f], { from: [0, 0], to: [0, 1] }))
            .roundRect(0.5, 0.5, 419, 259, 28, 0.6)
            .stroke({ color: 0xffffff, alpha: 0.08, width: 1 });
        const caption = new SilkText({ text: 'Frame 1', fontFamily: font, fontSize: 11, fill: 0x8e8e93 });

        caption.position.set(2, -18);
        world.addChild(card, caption);

        const title = new TextEditor({
            text: 'Edit me',
            fontFamily: font,
            fontSize: 30,
            fontWeight: 700,
            fill: 0xffffff,
            multiline: false,
            history,
            element: app.canvas,
        });
        const body = new TextEditor({
            text: 'Text on an infinite canvas. Zoom to 800% and the caret is still one crisp pixel and a half wide.\n\nDouble click a word, triple click a paragraph.',
            fontFamily: font,
            fontSize: 15,
            lineHeight: 22,
            fill: 0xc7c7cc,
            width: 356,
            placeholder: 'Write something',
            history,
            element: app.canvas,
        });

        title.position.set(32, 30);
        body.position.set(32, 86);
        world.addChild(title, body);

        // a tiny caption follows the title as you type
        const count = new SilkText({ fontFamily: font, fontSize: 11, fill: 0x636366, tabularNumbers: true });

        count.position.set(32, 230);
        world.addChild(count);
        const update = () => {
            const words = body.value.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;

            count.text = `${title.value.length} characters in the title, ${words} words below`;
        };
        const stops = [title.state.text.subscribe(update), body.state.text.subscribe(update), bindHistoryKeys(history)];

        update();
        const place = () =>
            zoom.position.set(app.screen.width - zoom.size[0] - 12, app.screen.height - zoom.size[1] - 12);

        app.renderer.on('resize', place);
        place();
        camera.fit({ x: -20, y: -40, width: 460, height: 320 }, 24, false);

        return () => {
            for (const stop of stops) stop();
            app.renderer.off('resize', place);
            title.destroy();
            body.destroy();
            camera.destroy();
        };
    },
});
```

```ts

declare const world: Container;
declare const canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;

const note = new TextEditor({
    text: 'Edit me',
    fontSize: 15,
    width: 320, // wraps at 320, grows in height
    placeholder: 'Write something',
    element: canvas,
});

note.on('change', (text: string) => console.log('saved', text));
world.addChild(note);
```

The note wraps at 320 units and grows downwards as you type. `change` fires when the edit is
committed with a new value. Every SilkText style option works too: font, size, weight, fill,
letter spacing, line height and alignment.

Words for double click and word jumps come from `Intl.Segmenter`, so they follow the language.
Arrow keys move by grapheme, so an emoji or an accented letter is one step.

### How typing reaches the canvas

The keys do not go to the canvas. On focus, the editor puts a hidden `<textarea>` on the page at
the caret and keeps its text and selection in step. The browser types into it, and the editor
applies each change.

That is why the platform's own input works. Input methods for Japanese, Chinese and Korean show
the composed text inline with an underline, and it becomes one undo step when confirmed. Dead
keys, mobile keyboards, autocorrect, the emoji picker and the system clipboard work too.

The hidden field follows the caret as you type, pan or zoom, so input method popups open next to
the text. Pass `element` (usually `app.canvas`) to place it. Without it, the editor uses the
element of the first press.

### Build a form

Live demo `text-edit-form` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { watch } from 'pixi-silk/signal';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { TextEditor } from 'pixi-silk/ui/textedit';
import { defineDemo } from './runtime';

// Single-line fields drawn on the canvas. Tab moves to the next field, Enter in the last one sends
// the form. Japanese or Chinese input, dead keys and the emoji picker type into them as usual.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Text form',
    size: [520, 330],
    setup({ silk, stage }) {
        const font = 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif';
        const fields: TextEditor[] = [];
        const stops: (() => void)[] = [];
        const field = (label: string, y: number, options: ConstructorParameters<typeof TextEditor>[0]) => {
            const row = new Container();
            const name = new SilkText({ text: label, fontFamily: font, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, fill: 0x8e8e93 });
            const box = new SilkGraphics();
            const editor = new TextEditor({
                fontFamily: font,
                fontSize: 15,
                fill: 0xf2f2f7,
                multiline: false,
                width: 276,
                element: silk.app.canvas,
                ...options,
            });

            editor.position.set(12, 31);
            // a press anywhere on the box focuses the field
            box.eventMode = 'static';
            box.cursor = 'text';
            box.on('pointerdown', (e) => {
                (e.nativeEvent as Event).preventDefault();
                editor.focus();
            });
            row.addChild(name, box, editor);
            row.position.set(40, y);
            // the box lights up while its field has focus
            stops.push(
                watch(editor.state.focused, (on) => {
                    box.clear()
                        .roundRect(0, 20, 300, 40, 10, 0.6)
                        .fill(on ? 0x1c1c22 : 0x18181b)
                        .roundRect(0, 20, 300, 40, 10, 0.6)
                        .stroke({ color: on ? 0x0a84ff : 0x3a3a3c, width: on ? 1.5 : 1, alignment: 'inside' });
                }),
            );
            stage.addChild(row);
            fields.push(editor);

            return editor;
        };

        const name = field('Name', 24, { placeholder: 'Ada Lovelace' });
        const email = field('Email', 104, { placeholder: 'ada@example.com' });
        const code = field('Promo code, 8 characters at most', 184, { placeholder: 'SILK2026', maxLength: 8 });
        const result = new SilkText({
            fontFamily: font,
            fontSize: 12,
            fill: 0x30d158,
            wordWrap: true,
            wordWrapWidth: 150,
        });
        const button = new Container();
        const pill = new SilkGraphics().pill(0, 0, 140, 40).fill(0x0a84ff);
        const label = new SilkText({ text: 'Send', fontFamily: font, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, anchor: 0.5 });

        label.position.set(70, 20);
        button.addChild(pill, label);
        button.position.set(360, 228);
        button.eventMode = 'static';
        button.cursor = 'pointer';
        result.position.set(360, 52);
        stage.addChild(button, result);
        const send = () => {
            const values = [name.value || 'no name', email.value || 'no email', code.value || 'no code'];

            result.text = `Sent\n${values.join('\n')}`;
            TextEditor.active?.blur();
        };

        button.on('pointertap', send);
        code.on('submit', send);
        // Enter in the first fields moves on, like a sign-up form
        name.on('submit', () => email.focus(true));
        email.on('submit', () => code.focus(true));
        stops.push(TextEditor.chain(fields));

        return () => {
            for (const stop of stops) stop();
            for (const f of fields) f.destroy();
        };
    },
});
```

With `multiline: false` the editor is an input. Enter commits instead of breaking the line, and a
`width` clips the text and scrolls it to keep the caret in view. `TextEditor.chain()` links fields in
Tab order.

```ts

const email = new TextEditor({ multiline: false, width: 260, placeholder: 'ada@example.com' });
const code = new TextEditor({ multiline: false, width: 260, maxLength: 8 });

TextEditor.chain([email, code]);
code.on('submit', () => console.log(email.value, code.value));
```

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `multiline` | true | A text area, or a single-line input |
| `width` | none | Wraps a text area, clips and scrolls an input. Without it the box grows with the text |
| `placeholder` | empty | Shown while the text is empty |
| `maxLength` | none | Longest text in UTF-16 code units, like `maxlength`. Never splits an emoji |
| `readOnly` | false | Selectable and copyable, not editable |
| `selectable` | true | False turns pointer input off |
| `enter` | `newline`, or `commit` for one line | `newline`, `commit` or `blur`. Shift + Enter breaks the line in a text area |
| `tab` | `next` | `next` emits `tab` and leaves, `insert` types a tab |
| `history` | its own | A `HistoryLike` to share the app's undo stack |
| `ariaLabel` | the placeholder | Name of the field for screen readers |
| `handles` | true | Drag handles at the ends of a touch selection |
| `caretWidth` | 1.5 | Caret width in screen pixels, whatever the zoom |
| `theme` | blue | Caret, selection and composition colours |

In a multi-line field, cmd or ctrl + Enter commits and leaves. Escape leaves any field.

### Show text people can copy

Live demo `text-edit-readonly` (source):

```ts
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { TextEditor } from 'pixi-silk/ui/textedit';
import { defineDemo } from './runtime';

// A read-only paragraph: select with the mouse or a long press, cmd or ctrl + C copies.
// There is no caret and typing does nothing.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Read-only text selection',
    size: [520, 260],
    setup({ silk, stage }) {
        const font = 'Inter, system-ui, sans-serif';
        const paragraph = new TextEditor({
            text: 'Release notes. Text layers now edit on the canvas. Selection, the clipboard and input methods go through a hidden text field, so they behave the way the platform does. Double click a word to select it.',
            fontFamily: font,
            fontSize: 15,
            lineHeight: 23,
            fill: 0xd1d1d6,
            width: 440,
            readOnly: true,
            element: silk.app.canvas,
            theme: { selection: 0xffd60a, selectionAlpha: 0.28 },
        });
        const readout = new SilkText({ fontFamily: font, fontSize: 12, fill: 0x8e8e93 });

        paragraph.position.set(40, 36);
        readout.position.set(40, 214);
        stage.addChild(paragraph, readout);
        const stop = paragraph.state.selection.subscribe(() => {
            const n = paragraph.selectedText.length;

            readout.text = n ? `${n} characters selected. Cmd or ctrl + C copies them.` : '';
        });

        return () => {
            stop();
            paragraph.destroy();
        };
    },
});
```

`readOnly: true` keeps clicking, selecting and copying, and drops the caret and editing. Use it for
release notes, logs or error messages.

### Events, signals and undo

The editor emits `focus`, `blur`, `input` on every edit, `change` on a commit with a new value,
`submit` on Enter in a single-line field, and `tab`. The same state is on `editor.state` as
[signals](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/radial-menus-and-buttons/#state-in-signals): `text`, `committed`, `focused`,
`selection` and `composing`.

Undo is built in. Consecutive typing folds into one step, and a click or an arrow key starts the
next. To keep text edits in the same stack as the rest of an editor, pass its history:

```ts

const history = new History();
const title = new TextEditor({ text: 'Frame 1', multiline: false, history });

bindHistoryKeys(history); // cmd + Z outside the field undoes text edits too
```

### Find a caret from a point

The caret math lives in the text layout, so other tools can use it. `caretAt(point)` returns the
caret nearest a local point. `caretRect(index)` gives where a caret sits and `rangeRects(from, to)`
the highlight boxes of a range. They follow wrapped lines, alignment, letter spacing and trailing
spaces.

```ts

const label = new SilkText({ text: 'Hello world', wordWrap: true, wordWrapWidth: 60 });
const hit = label.caretAt({ x: 30, y: 8 });
const caret = label.caretRect(hit.index, hit.upstream);

console.log(hit.index, caret.x, label.rangeRects(0, 5));
```

`pixi-silk/text` also exports these as functions on a `TextLayout`, with `graphemeStops`,
`wordAt` and the word jumps.

### Where it stops

Right-to-left and mixed-direction text is not supported. Arabic and Hebrew lay out left to right,
and the caret moves in source order. Emoji count as one caret step but draw as one-colour
silhouettes, and there are no ligatures, as in [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/text/#where-it-stops).

Spell check underlines do not show, because the hidden field is invisible.

---

# Radial menus and buttons

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/radial-menus-and-buttons/
Area: Interface

> RadialMenu is a marking menu with nested rings and per-device tuning. SilkButton animates in and out and ignores input while it moves or is busy.

A radial menu puts its items in a ring around the pointer, so a press, a drag towards an item and
a release pick it in one gesture. A button here is a widget that knows when it should not take a press.

### Open a radial menu

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `radial-menu` (source):

```ts
import { Container, Text } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { SilkButton } from 'pixi-silk/ui/button';
import { bindRadialTrigger, type RadialItem, RadialMenu } from 'pixi-silk/ui/radial';
import { defineDemo, FONT, INK, label, PALETTE } from './runtime';

// Vector icons: drawn once in white around (0, 0); the menu tints them.
type Icon = (g: SilkGraphics, s: number) => void;
const pointer: Icon = (g, s) => {
    g.triangle(-s * 0.3, -s * 0.42, s * 0.34, s * 0.06, -s * 0.06, s * 0.16, 2).fill(0xffffff);
    g.line(-s * 0.02, s * 0.12, s * 0.16, s * 0.42).stroke({ width: s * 0.12, cap: 'round', color: 0xffffff });
};
const move: Icon = (g, s) => {
    const r = s * 0.42;

    g.line(-r, 0, r, 0).stroke({ width: s * 0.09, cap: 'round', color: 0xffffff });
    g.line(0, -r, 0, r).stroke({ width: s * 0.09, cap: 'round', color: 0xffffff });
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        const a = (i * Math.PI) / 2;

        g.regularPoly(Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r, s * 0.14, 3, a + Math.PI / 2, 1).fill(0xffffff);
    }
};
const rotate: Icon = (g, s) => {
    g.arcSweep(0, 0, s * 0.32, -Math.PI * 0.35, Math.PI * 1.6).stroke({
        width: s * 0.1,
        cap: 'round',
        color: 0xffffff,
    });
    g.regularPoly(s * 0.18, -s * 0.3, s * 0.14, 3, Math.PI / 2, 1).fill(0xffffff);
};
const scale: Icon = (g, s) => {
    g.roundRect(-s * 0.4, -s * 0.1, s * 0.5, s * 0.5, 3).stroke({ width: s * 0.08, color: 0xffffff });
    g.roundRect(-s * 0.1, -s * 0.42, s * 0.52, s * 0.52, 3).fill(0xffffff);
};
const gear: Icon = (g, s) => {
    g.circle(0, 0, s * 0.33).stroke({ width: s * 0.16, dash: [s * 0.13, s * 0.13], color: 0xffffff });
    g.circle(0, 0, s * 0.22).stroke({ width: s * 0.1, color: 0xffffff });
};
const more: Icon = (g, s) => {
    for (const x of [-1, 0, 1]) g.circle(x * s * 0.28, 0, s * 0.09).fill(0xffffff);
};
const heart: Icon = (g, s) => g.heart(0, 0, s * 0.8, 0.1).fill(0xffffff);
const star: Icon = (g, s) => g.star(0, 0, 5, s * 0.45, undefined, 0, 1.5).fill(0xffffff);
const plus: Icon = (g, s) => {
    g.line(-s * 0.35, 0, s * 0.35, 0).stroke({ width: s * 0.13, cap: 'round', color: 0xffffff });
    g.line(0, -s * 0.35, 0, s * 0.35).stroke({ width: s * 0.13, cap: 'round', color: 0xffffff });
};

const tools: RadialItem[] = [
    { id: 'select', label: 'SELECT', icon: pointer },
    {
        id: 'move',
        label: 'MOVE',
        icon: move,
        children: [
            { id: 'intuitive', label: 'Intuitive' },
            { id: 'axis', label: 'Axis' },
            { id: 'free', label: 'Free' },
        ],
    },
    {
        id: 'rotate',
        label: 'ROTATE',
        icon: rotate,
        children: [
            { id: 'uniform', label: 'Uniform' },
            { id: 'axis', label: 'Axis' },
            { id: 'free', label: 'Free' },
        ],
    },
    { id: 'scale', label: 'SCALE', icon: scale },
    { id: 'settings', label: 'SETTINGS', icon: gear, disabled: true },
    { id: 'more', label: 'MORE', icon: more },
];

// Press anywhere (or the round button) and drag to an item, or tap to keep the menu open.
// The `+` button opens a half ring that turns away from the edge it sits on. The artboard
// on the right is only scenery: the menus pick tools, they do not edit it.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Radial menu',
    size: [720, 400],
    touchAction: 'none',
    controls: {
        closeOnLeave: { type: 'toggle', label: 'close on leave', value: false },
    },
    setup({ silk, stage, params, tick }) {
        const ticker = silk.app.ticker;
        const surface = new Container();
        const status = new Text({
            text: 'press, drag, release, or tap',
            style: { fill: INK.muted, fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: 13 },
        });
        const bounds = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 720, height: 400 };
        const report = (_item: RadialItem, path: readonly RadialItem[]) => {
            status.text = `picked  ${path.map((i) => i.id).join(' › ')}`;
        };
        const menu = new RadialMenu({ items: tools, bounds, ticker, onSelect: report });
        const quick = new RadialMenu({
            items: [
                { id: 'heart', label: 'LIKE', icon: heart },
                { id: 'star', label: 'FAVOURITE', icon: star },
                { id: 'plus', label: 'ADD', icon: plus },
                { id: 'more', label: 'MORE', icon: more },
            ],
            sweep: Math.PI * 0.9,
            innerRadius: 34,
            outerRadius: 92,
            bounds,
            ticker,
            onSelect: report,
        });
        const hub = new SilkButton({ shape: 'circle', height: 64, icon: gear, iconSize: 28, ticker });
        const add = new SilkButton({ shape: 'circle', height: 52, icon: plus, iconSize: 22, ticker });

        // scenery: an artboard with a few layers, as in a drawing app
        const board = new SilkGraphics()
            .roundRect(330, 70, 300, 250, 16, 0.6)
            .fill(INK.surface)
            .stroke({ width: 1, color: INK.line, alignment: 'inside' })
            .roundRect(360, 104, 120, 84, 14, 0.6)
            .fill(PALETTE.blue)
            .circle(550, 150, 44)
            .fill(PALETTE.pink)
            .star(420, 258, 5, 40, undefined, 0, 4)
            .fill(PALETTE.yellow)
            .roundRect(500, 224, 100, 64, 32)
            .fill(PALETTE.green);

        surface.eventMode = 'static';
        surface.hitArea = { contains: (x, y) => x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x <= 720 && y <= 400 };
        status.position.set(24, 20);
        hub.position.set(170, 200);
        add.position.set(670, 350);
        stage.addChild(
            surface,
            board,
            label('Artboard', 330, 46),
            label('tools', 170, 244, {}, 0.5),
            label('quick add', 634, 342, {}, 1),
            status,
            hub,
            add,
            menu,
            quick,
        );

        bindRadialTrigger(surface, menu, { at: 'pointer' });
        bindRadialTrigger(hub, menu);
        bindRadialTrigger(add, quick);
        menu.state.hover.subscribe((item) => {
            if (item) status.text = `${menu.state.kind.value}: ${item.id}${item.disabled ? ' (disabled)' : ''}`;
        });

        tick(() => {
            menu.closeOnLeave = quick.closeOnLeave = params.closeOnLeave;
        });

        return undefined;
    },
});
```

```ts

const menu = new RadialMenu({
    items: [
        { id: 'select', label: 'SELECT', icon: drawPointer },
        { id: 'move', label: 'MOVE', icon: drawMove, children: [{ id: 'axis', label: 'Axis' }, { id: 'free', label: 'Free' }] },
        { id: 'delete', label: 'DELETE', icon: drawTrash, disabled: true },
    ],
    sweep: Math.PI * 2, // Math.PI for a half ring
    bounds: { x: 0, y: 0, width: app.screen.width, height: app.screen.height },
    onSelect: (item, path) => console.log(path.map((i) => i.id).join(' > ')),
});

app.stage.addChild(menu);
bindRadialTrigger(toolButton, menu); // or menu.openAt(x, y, event)
```

A press on `toolButton` opens the menu at the pointer. `onSelect` gets the item and the path to it
through the rings, so a pick in the second ring logs `move > axis`.

The menu picks by angle. Past a small dead zone the whole wedge counts, out to the falloff
distance, so a fast flick lands on its item even when the pointer overshoots the ring. The current
item holds until the pointer is clearly past its edge, so a shaky finger on a boundary does not
flicker between two items.

Items with `children` open the next ring around themselves, to any depth. A full ring slides to
stay inside `bounds`, and a partial ring turns to face the inside instead.

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `innerRadius`, `outerRadius` | 40, 100 | The first ring |
| `sweep` | full turn | Angle of the first ring, `Math.PI` for a half |
| `direction` | up | Where the ring faces, in radians |
| `closeOnLeave` | false | Close once the pointer stays outside the falloff area. By default the menu stays |
| `closeOnSelect` | true | Close after a pick |
| `selectParents` | false | Items with children can be picked too |
| `profiles` | per pointer kind | Dead zone, falloff, scale, tap time and hysteresis for mouse, pen and touch |
| `hub` | true | Draw the centre disc |

Touch gets bigger targets and more forgiveness than a mouse. Override any of it with `profiles`.

Icons are a string, a texture, a container or a function that draws with SilkGraphics in white.
The menu tints them, so a hover colour costs no redraw.

### Make a button that waits

```ts

const play = new SilkButton({
    shape: 'circle',
    height: 76,
    icon: drawPlay,
    holdMs: 450,
    onPress: () => console.log('play'),
    onHold: () => console.log('play on repeat'),
});
const share = new SilkButton({ label: 'Share' });

await share.hide();        // resolves when the animation ends
play.state.busy.set(true); // spinner, input off
play.state.active.subscribe((on) => console.log('accepts input', on));
```

A button accepts input only when it is shown, settled, enabled and not busy. That is what keeps a
save button from being pressed twice while it saves. `show()` and `hide()` return promises that resolve `false`
if the animation was reversed half way. A press that slides a little off the button still counts
on release, within `hitPadding` (6 by default).

`ButtonDock` from `pixi-silk/ui/dock` lines buttons up and closes the gap when one hides. Each
button's space follows its animated appearance, so the reflow and the fade move together.

### State in signals

Every widget keeps its state in `state`: small signals you can read, write and subscribe to.
`pixi-silk/signal` has no Pixi code in it. A signal is a value with `subscribe`, with no
dependency tracking, so it plugs into React, MobX or plain callbacks.

```ts

const volume = signal(1);
const label = computed([volume], () => `volume ${volume.value}`);

volume.subscribe((now, before) => console.log(before, '->', now));
batch(() => {
    volume.set(2);
    volume.set(3);
}); // one notification
```

`computed` takes its sources as a list, so it recomputes only when one of them changes. Widgets
animate on `Ticker.shared` and leave the ticker once nothing moves. Pass `ticker` to use your
application's ticker, or `null` to step them yourself with `tick(dt)`.

---

# Layout and smart containers

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/layout/
Area: Interface

> Place blocks on portrait and landscape screens with SmartContainer records and breakpoints, then lay out what is inside them with flexbox. pixi-silk/ui/layout.

`SmartContainer` places a whole block on the screen, such as a corner readout, the main view or a
bottom bar, on portrait and landscape screens. `FlexContainer` arranges children inside a block with flexbox. Both come from
`pixi-silk/ui/layout`.

### Place blocks on portrait and landscape screens

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `layout-hud` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics, vertical } from 'pixi-silk';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import {
    DEVICES,
    FlexContainer,
    layoutDebug,
    SmartContainer,
    type SmartContainerOptions,
    simulateViewport,
} from 'pixi-silk/ui/layout';
import { defineDemo } from './runtime';

// A media player HUD built from SmartContainers, each placed by a portrait and a landscape record.
// The track title pins to the top left corner, the menu to the top right, the artwork stays
// centred and fits, and the bar stretches along the bottom with a flex row inside. Pick a device:
// the page previews it letterboxed, and the HUD glides to the new breakpoint. Press L, or tick
// debug, for the layout overlay. Hover a box for its readout. Press play to run the clock.

const font = 'system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, sans-serif';

/** Cover art for the track: a sun over hills, 1040x640 design units. */
function artwork(): Container {
    const g = new SilkGraphics();

    g.roundRect(0, 0, 1040, 640, 40, 0.6)
        .fill({ gradient: vertical([0x1e1b4b, 0x7c3aed, 0xf472b6]) })
        .circle(520, 360, 170)
        .fill({ gradient: vertical([0xffd60a, 0xff9f0a]) });
    // the hills are cut to the card
    g.boolean(
        'intersect',
        (g) => g.roundRect(0, 0, 1040, 640, 40, 0.6),
        (g) => g.ellipse(260, 640, 420, 190),
    ).fill(0x2e1065);
    g.boolean(
        'intersect',
        (g) => g.roundRect(0, 0, 1040, 640, 40, 0.6),
        (g) => g.ellipse(820, 660, 420, 220),
    ).fill(0x3b0764);
    g.roundRect(0, 0, 1040, 640, 40, 0.6).stroke({ width: 2, color: 0xffffff, alpha: 0.12, alignment: 'inside' });

    return g;
}

/** A pill with a label and a value. The value shrinks to fit the pill, never below 0.55. */
function pill(label: string, value: string, color: number): { box: FlexContainer; value: SilkText } {
    const box = new FlexContainer({ layout: { direction: 'column', padding: [14, 28], gap: 2 } });
    const bg = new SilkGraphics();
    const v = new SilkText({ text: value, fontFamily: font, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 60, fill: color });

    box.add(bg, {
        position: 'absolute',
        inset: 0,
        onSize: (w, h) => bg.clear().roundRect(0, 0, w, h, 30, 0.6).fill(0x1c1c1e),
    });
    box.add(new SilkText({ text: label, fontFamily: font, fontSize: 34, fill: 0xa1a1aa }));
    box.add(v, { fit: 'scale-down', minScale: 0.55 });

    return { box, value: v };
}

/** A round button with a label on it. */
function button(radius: number, color: number, text: string): Container {
    const c = new Container();

    c.addChild(
        new SilkGraphics().circle(0, 0, radius).fill(color),
        new SilkText({ text, fontFamily: font, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: radius * 0.36, fill: 0xffffff, anchor: 0.5 }),
    );
    c.eventMode = 'static';
    c.cursor = 'pointer';

    return c;
}

const clock = (s: number) => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(s % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
const LENGTH = 238;

export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Responsive player HUD',
    size: [920, 520],
    controls: {
        device: { type: 'select', label: 'device', options: Object.keys(DEVICES), value: 'desktop' },
        notch: { type: 'toggle', label: 'notch', value: false },
        debug: { type: 'toggle', label: 'debug', value: false },
    },
    setup({ silk, stage, params, tick }) {
        // a device window previewed inside the page. Every SmartContainer follows it.
        const sim = simulateViewport({ area: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 920, height: 520 }, device: 'desktop' });
        const debug = layoutDebug(silk.app, { key: 'KeyL' });
        const all: SmartContainer[] = [];
        const make = (label: string, options: SmartContainerOptions) => {
            const c = new SmartContainer({
                ticker: silk.app.ticker,
                viewport: sim,
                // apps usually snap. The demo glides on the switch to show where things go.
                switchTransition: { stiffness: 160, damping: 22 },
                ...options,
            });

            c.label = label;
            all.push(c);
            sim.content.addChild(c);

            return c;
        };

        // background: covers the whole window, under the notch too
        const bg = make('background', {
            portraitData: { width: 1080, height: 1920, fitCover: true, ignoreSafeArea: true },
            landscapeData: { width: 1920, height: 1080, fitCover: true, ignoreSafeArea: true },
        });

        bg.addChild(new SilkGraphics().rect(0, 0, 1920, 1920).fill(0x111113));

        // artwork: centred, fit to the reference resolution, moved up to leave room for the bar
        make('artwork', {
            portraitData: { width: 1040, height: 640, correctionOffsetY: -200 },
            landscapeData: { width: 1040, height: 640, correctionOffsetY: -50 },
        }).addChild(artwork());

        // track title pinned to the top left corner, menu to the top right
        const corner = make('title', {
            layout: { padding: 24 },
            portraitData: { align: 'left', valign: 'top', width: 440, height: 170 },
            landscapeData: { align: 'left', valign: 'top', width: 440, height: 170 },
        });

        corner.add(pill('NOW PLAYING', 'Night Drive', 0xffffff).box, { grow: 1 });

        make('menu', {
            layout: { justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center' },
            portraitData: { align: 'right', valign: 'top', width: 140, height: 140 },
            landscapeData: { align: 'right', valign: 'top', width: 140, height: 140 },
        }).add(button(40, 0x2c2c2e, 'MENU'));

        // the bar: along the bottom and as wide as the window, with a flex row inside
        const bar = make('bar', {
            layout: {
                padding: [16, 32, 28],
                gap: 24,
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                media: [{ breakpoint: 'portrait', style: { wrap: 'wrap', justifyContent: 'center', rowGap: 16 } }],
            },
            portraitData: { valign: 'bottom', width: 1080, height: 420, extendWidth: true },
            landscapeData: { valign: 'bottom', width: 1920, height: 200, extendWidth: true },
        });
        const time = pill('TIME', `0:00 / ${clock(LENGTH)}`, 0xffffff);
        const volume = pill('VOLUME', '72%', 0x64d2ff);
        const play = button(76, 0x30d158, 'PLAY');
        const playLabel = play.children[1] as SilkText;
        let playing = false;
        let position = 0;

        // in portrait the play button takes a row of its own above the two pills
        bar.add(play, { media: [{ breakpoint: 'portrait', style: { basis: '100%' } }] });
        bar.add(time.box, { grow: 1, basis: 0, maxWidth: 440 });
        bar.add(volume.box, { grow: 1, basis: 0, maxWidth: 440 });

        play.on('pointertap', () => {
            playing = !playing;
            playLabel.text = playing ? 'PAUSE' : 'PLAY';
        });

        stage.addChild(sim);

        let last = '';
        let debugParam = false;

        tick((_t, dt) => {
            if (playing) {
                const before = Math.floor(position);

                position = (position + dt) % LENGTH;
                if (Math.floor(position) !== before) {
                    time.value.text = `${clock(position)} / ${clock(LENGTH)}`;
                    // new text, new size: measure it again
                    time.box.invalidate(time.value);
                }
            }
            // the checkbox and the L key both toggle the overlay
            if (params.debug !== debugParam) {
                debugParam = params.debug;
                if (debug.enabled !== debugParam) debug.toggle();
            }
            const key = `${params.device}|${params.notch}`;

            if (key === last) return;
            last = key;
            const [w, h] = DEVICES[params.device];

            for (const c of all)
                c.setSafeArea(params.notch ? (w > h ? { left: 44, right: 44 } : { top: 47, bottom: 34 }) : {});
            sim.setDevice(params.device);
        });

        return () => {
            debug.destroy();
            for (const c of all) c.destroy({ children: true });
        };
    },
});
```

The screen above is five SmartContainers. One readout pins to the top left and the menu to the top
right. The main view stays centred, the bar runs along the bottom and the background covers the
whole window.

```ts

const main = new SmartContainer({
    viewport: app,
    portraitData: { width: 1040, height: 640, correctionOffsetY: -200 },
    landscapeData: { width: 1040, height: 640, correctionOffsetY: -50 },
});

app.stage.addChild(main);
```

The container follows `app` and lays out again on every resize. It uses `portraitData` when the
window is taller than wide and `landscapeData` otherwise. Without a `viewport` a SmartContainer
does nothing: it has no window to place itself in.

The scale is the window size over the reference resolution, the smaller ratio, or the larger one
with `fitCover`. Stretch replaces it per axis, and the corrections apply last. `placeBox(record, window)` gives the same placement without a container.

#### Layout records

| Field | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `width`, `height` | 0 | The design box, in design units |
| `viewportWidth`, `viewportHeight` | 1080x1920 portrait, 1920x1080 landscape | The reference resolution |
| `align`, `valign` | `center` | Anchor of the scaled box: `left`, `center`, `right` and `top`, `center`, `bottom` |
| `fitCover` | false | Cover the window instead of fitting inside it |
| `stretchWidth`, `stretchHeight` | false | Scale one axis to span the window. Distorts the content, which suits backgrounds |
| `extendWidth`, `extendHeight` | false | Keep the uniform scale and grow the box to the window. A flex layout spreads out |
| `correctionScaleX`, `correctionScaleY` | 1 | Multiply the scale |
| `correctionOffsetX`, `correctionOffsetY` | 0 | Move the box, in design units |
| `ignoreSafeArea` | false | Place the box in the whole window, under notches |
| `layout` | the container's | Flex style for the children while this record is active |

#### Container options

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `viewport` | none | An Application or renderer (follows `resize`), a signal of a rectangle, or a plain rectangle (call `updateContainer()` after a change) |
| `portraitData`, `landscapeData` | none | The two orientation records |
| `breakpoints` | none | Named records with a `when(width, height)` test, tried in order before the orientations |
| `safeArea` | none | Insets in screen pixels that shrink the window boxes are placed in |
| `switchTransition` | false | Glide on a spring to the new place and scale when the breakpoint changes. Resizes inside one breakpoint snap |
| `layout` | none | Turns the design box into a flex container |

```ts

const background = new SmartContainer({
    viewport: app.renderer,
    safeArea: { top: 44, bottom: 34 },
    breakpoints: [
        { name: 'ultrawide', when: (w, h) => w / h > 2.1, data: { width: 2560, height: 1080, viewportWidth: 2560, fitCover: true } },
    ],
    portraitData: { width: 1080, height: 1920, fitCover: true, ignoreSafeArea: true },
    landscapeData: { width: 1920, height: 1080, fitCover: true, ignoreSafeArea: true },
});
```

On a window wider than 2.1:1 the `ultrawide` record wins. Otherwise the orientation decides.

#### Lay out the children of a box

Without a `layout`, children stay where you put them, in design units. With one, the design box is
a flex container, and `media` rules can switch its style per breakpoint:

```ts

const bar = new SmartContainer({
    viewport: app,
    layout: {
        padding: [16, 32, 28],
        gap: 24,
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        media: [{ breakpoint: 'portrait', style: { wrap: 'wrap', justifyContent: 'center' } }],
    },
    portraitData: { valign: 'bottom', width: 1080, height: 420, extendWidth: true },
    landscapeData: { valign: 'bottom', width: 1920, height: 200, extendWidth: true },
});
const play = new Container();

bar.add(play, { media: [{ breakpoint: 'portrait', style: { basis: '100%' } }] });
```

### Arrange children with flexbox

Live demo `layout-stack` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import type { AlignItems, FlexDirection, JustifyContent } from 'pixi-silk/layout';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { FlexContainer, layoutDebug } from 'pixi-silk/ui/layout';
import { defineDemo, INK, PAD, SERIES } from './runtime';

// An auto-layout stack, like a Figma frame. Change the item count, direction, gap, padding and
// alignment: cards glide to their new places on springs. The debug overlay draws the boxes and
// cells (blue), the padding (green), the gaps (pink) and real bounds (white). Tick debug, then
// hover a card.

const COLORS = SERIES;
const W = 720;
const H = 420;

/** A card of its own size that redraws at its cell size, so `align: stretch` shows. */
function card(frame: FlexContainer, i: number): Container {
    const c = new Container();
    const g = new SilkGraphics();
    const t = new SilkText({ text: String(i + 1), fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 22, fill: 0xffffff, anchor: 0.5 });

    c.addChild(g, t);

    return frame.add(c, {
        minWidth: 92 + (i % 3) * 16,
        minHeight: 64 + (i % 2) * 16,
        onSize: (w, h) => {
            g.clear()
                .roundRect(0, 0, w, h, 14, 0.6)
                .fill(COLORS[i % COLORS.length]);
            t.position.set(w / 2, h / 2);
        },
    });
}

export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Flex stack',
    size: [W, H],
    controls: {
        items: { type: 'range', label: 'items', min: 1, max: 14, step: 1, value: 8 },
        direction: {
            type: 'select',
            label: 'direction',
            options: ['row', 'column', 'row-reverse', 'column-reverse'],
            value: 'row',
        },
        wrap: { type: 'toggle', label: 'wrap', value: true },
        gap: { type: 'range', label: 'gap', min: 0, max: 40, step: 1, value: 12, unit: 'px' },
        padding: { type: 'range', label: 'padding', min: 0, max: 60, step: 1, value: 24, unit: 'px' },
        justify: {
            type: 'select',
            label: 'justify',
            options: ['start', 'center', 'end', 'space-between', 'space-around', 'space-evenly'],
            value: 'center',
        },
        align: { type: 'select', label: 'align', options: ['start', 'center', 'end', 'stretch'], value: 'center' },
        debug: { type: 'toggle', label: 'debug', value: false },
    },
    setup({ silk, stage, params, tick }) {
        const frame = new FlexContainer({ ticker: silk.app.ticker, transition: true });
        // the layout debug overlay: boxes, padding, gaps, cells against real bounds. Hover a card.
        const debug = layoutDebug(silk.app, { enabled: false });
        const bg = new SilkGraphics();
        const cards: Container[] = [];
        let last = '';

        frame.resize(W - 2 * PAD, H - 2 * PAD);
        frame.position.set(PAD, PAD);
        frame.add(bg, {
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            animate: false,
            onSize: (w, h) => bg.clear().roundRect(0, 0, w, h, 20, 0.6).fill(INK.surface),
        });
        stage.addChild(frame);

        tick(() => {
            const key = JSON.stringify(params);

            if (key === last) return;
            last = key;
            while (cards.length < params.items) cards.push(card(frame, cards.length));
            while (cards.length > params.items) cards.pop()?.destroy({ children: true });
            frame.setStyle({
                direction: params.direction as FlexDirection,
                wrap: params.wrap ? 'wrap' : 'nowrap',
                gap: params.gap,
                padding: params.padding,
                justifyContent: params.justify as JustifyContent,
                alignItems: params.align as AlignItems,
                alignContent: 'center',
            });
            if (debug.enabled !== params.debug) debug.toggle();
        });

        return () => {
            debug.destroy();
            frame.destroy({ children: true });
        };
    },
});
```

```ts

const row = new FlexContainer({ layout: { gap: 12, padding: 16, alignItems: 'center' } });

row.add(Sprite.from('coin.png'), { width: 48, height: 48, fit: 'contain' });
row.add(new Text({ text: 'Balance' }), { grow: 1 });
row.resize(480, 80);
```

The coin takes a 48 by 48 box and the label takes the rest of the row. Call `resize(width, height)`
on a root FlexContainer, or nest it in another FlexContainer or a SmartContainer. A SmartContainer
inside a FlexContainer places itself and takes no space.

Give a child a style with `add(child, style)` or `setLayout(child, style)`. A child is measured by
its local bounds, or by the style's `measure` (for wrapped text, use the `wrapText` helper). Pixi
`Text` and `SilkText` both report real text metrics. The style fields follow CSS flexbox and are
listed in [Layout engine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/layout-engine/#styles).

The container lays out once per frame, and only after a change: a child added, removed or
restyled, a new size, or `invalidate(child)` after new text or a new texture. An idle container is
off the ticker.

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `layout` | none | The container's own flex style |
| `transition` | false | Children glide to their new places on springs, scale included. `true` is stiffness 170, damping 26 |
| `watch` | false | Check the children's bounds every frame and lay out again when they change |
| `onLayout` | none | Called after every layout pass |
| `ticker` | `Ticker.shared` | `null` to call `tick()` yourself |

A child with `animate: false` never glides. A child with `onSize` is called with its box size
whenever that changes, which suits a panel background. Such a child measures as zero, so size it
with `inset`, `grow` or a size:

```ts

const panel = new FlexContainer({ layout: { padding: 16 } });
const bg = new SilkGraphics();

panel.add(bg, {
    position: 'absolute',
    inset: 0,
    onSize: (w, h) => bg.clear().roundRect(0, 0, w, h, 18, 0.6).fill(0x1c1c1e),
});
```

### Fit art and shrink text into a box

Live demo `layout-fit` (source):

```ts
import type { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { type FitMode, FlexContainer } from 'pixi-silk/ui/layout';
import { defineDemo, INK, PALETTE } from './runtime';

// The same 200x120 artwork in five boxes, one per fit mode, like CSS object-fit. Resize the
// boxes and move the alignment. The last box shrinks a long title to fit, down to a floor.

const MODES: FitMode[] = ['none', 'contain', 'cover', 'scale-down', 'fill'];
const ALIGN: Record<string, [number, number]> = { center: [0.5, 0.5], start: [0, 0], end: [1, 1] };

/** A 200x120 card: sky, sun and hills. */
function artwork(): Container {
    const g = new SilkGraphics();

    // everything stays inside the card, so its bounds are exactly 200x120
    g.roundRect(0, 0, 200, 120, 16, 0.6).fill(PALETTE.blue);
    g.circle(150, 38, 18).fill(PALETTE.yellow);
    g.poly([0, 84, 50, 62, 110, 86, 160, 70, 200, 82, 200, 104, 184, 120, 16, 120, 0, 104]).fill(PALETTE.green);

    return g;
}

export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Fit modes',
    size: [720, 360],
    controls: {
        width: { type: 'range', label: 'box width', min: 60, max: 200, step: 1, value: 170, unit: 'px' },
        height: { type: 'range', label: 'box height', min: 50, max: 120, step: 1, value: 100, unit: 'px' },
        align: { type: 'select', label: 'fitAlign', options: ['center', 'start', 'end'], value: 'center' },
    },
    setup({ silk, stage, params, tick }) {
        // two rows of three boxes
        const grid = new FlexContainer({
            ticker: silk.app.ticker,
            layout: { direction: 'column', gap: 18, padding: 24, alignItems: 'center' },
        });
        const rows = [0, 1].map(() =>
            grid.add(new FlexContainer({ layout: { gap: 24, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'start' } })),
        );
        const boxes: { box: FlexContainer; art: Container }[] = [];
        const label = (text: string) => new SilkText({ text, fontSize: 13, fill: INK.muted });
        const makeBox = (title: string) => {
            const column = rows[boxes.length < 3 ? 0 : 1].add(
```
