```ts
const eraser: Icon = (g, s) => g.roundRect(-s * 0.36, -s * 0.2, s * 0.72, s * 0.4, 4).fill(0xffffff);
const smudge: Icon = (g, s) => g.heart(0, 0, s * 0.7, 0.2).fill(0xffffff);
const fill: Icon = (g, s) => g.regularPoly(0, 0, s * 0.38, 6, 0, 3).fill(0xffffff);
const pick: Icon = (g, s) => {
    g.circle(0, 0, s * 0.3).stroke({ width: s * 0.1, color: 0xffffff });
    g.circle(0, 0, s * 0.08).fill(0xffffff);
};

// Hold Space over the canvas (or long-press it) for the mode ring, release on a mode.
// Tap Space to swap back to the previous mode. The pill below is a SegmentedSwitch.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Mode ring',
    size: [640, 360],
    touchAction: 'none',
    setup({ silk, stage, width, height }) {
        const ticker = silk.app.ticker;
        const status = new Text({
            text: '',
            style: { fill: 0xf2f2f7, fontFamily: 'system-ui, sans-serif', fontSize: 18, fontWeight: '600' },
        });
        const hint = new Text({
            text: 'hold Space or long-press, tap Space to swap back',
            style: { fill: 0x8e8e93, fontFamily: 'system-ui, sans-serif', fontSize: 13 },
        });
        const speed = new SegmentedSwitch({
            options: [
                { id: 'slow', label: 'Slow' },
                { id: 'normal', label: 'Normal' },
                { id: 'turbo', label: 'Turbo' },
            ],
            value: 'normal',
            ticker,
        });
        const tools = new SegmentedSwitch({
            options: [
                { id: 'brush', icon: brush },
                { id: 'eraser', icon: eraser },
                { id: 'smudge', icon: smudge },
                { id: 'fill', icon: fill },
                { id: 'pick', icon: pick },
            ],
            segmentWidth: 48,
            ticker,
        });
        const modes = new ModeSwitcher({
            modes: [
                { id: 'brush', label: 'BRUSH', icon: brush },
                { id: 'eraser', label: 'ERASER', icon: eraser },
                { id: 'smudge', label: 'SMUDGE', icon: smudge },
                { id: 'fill', label: 'FILL', icon: fill },
                { id: 'pick', label: 'PICKER', icon: pick },
            ],
            longPressTarget: stage,
            ticker,
        });

        stage.eventMode = 'static';
        stage.hitArea = { contains: (x, y) => x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x <= width && y <= height };
        status.anchor.set(0.5);
        hint.anchor.set(0.5);
        status.position.set(width / 2, 70);
        hint.position.set(width / 2, 100);
        tools.position.set(width / 2, 190);
        speed.position.set(width / 2, 290);
        stage.addChild(status, hint, tools, speed, modes);

        // the switcher and the toolbar share one source of truth
        const show = (mode: string) => {
            status.text = `mode: ${mode}`;
            tools.state.value.set(mode);
        };

        modes.state.mode.subscribe(show);
        tools.state.value.subscribe((id) => modes.set(id));
        show(modes.mode);

        return undefined;
    },
});
```

```ts

const modes = new ModeSwitcher({
    modes: [
        { id: 'brush', label: 'BRUSH', icon: drawPointer },
        { id: 'eraser', label: 'ERASER', icon: drawTrash },
    ],
    longPressTarget: app.stage,
    onChange: (mode, previous) => console.log(previous, mode),
});

app.stage.addChild(modes);
```

Hold `key` (Space by default) for 160 ms and a ring of modes opens under the pointer, most
recently used first. Release over a mode to switch. A quick tap swaps back to the previous mode
without showing anything. On touch screens a long-press on `longPressTarget` opens the same ring.

The key only acts while the pointer is over the canvas and no text field has focus. The ring is a
[radial menu](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/radial-menus-and-buttons/), styled through the `menu` option.

### Pick one of a few options

```ts

const speed = new SegmentedSwitch({
    options: [
        { id: 'slow', label: 'Slow' },
        { id: 'normal', label: 'Normal' },
        { id: 'turbo', label: 'Turbo' },
    ],
    value: 'normal',
});

speed.state.value.subscribe((id) => console.log(id));
```

A pill of choices with a thumb that slides to the selected one. Tap a segment, or press and slide
along the pill: the thumb follows the finger and the choice lands on release. Segments are at
least `segmentWidth` (64) wide and grow to fit their labels or icons.

# Dials and timeline

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/dials-and-timeline/
Area: Editor kit

> A jog wheel with fine control and inertia, scrub fields you drag sideways, and a compact animation timeline with keys, a loop range and zoom for PixiJS.

> **NOTE**
> Experimental in 1.0: the API may change in a minor release.

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `dial` (source):

```ts
import { JogDial, Scrubber } from 'pixi-silk/ui/dial';
import { defineDemo } from './runtime';

// Drag around a dial: the value keeps adding up over full turns, and further out turns slower.
// Flick it and it keeps turning. The right dial clicks through steps and stretches past its limits.
// Scrub the fields sideways. Shift is fine, Ctrl or Cmd snaps, a tap on either end steps.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Dials and number fields',
    size: [600, 300],
    touchAction: 'none',
    setup({ silk, stage }) {
        const ticker = silk.app.ticker;
        const endless = new JogDial({ ticker, radius: 62, turn: 360, format: (v) => `${Math.round(v)}°` });
        const stepped = new JogDial({
            ticker,
            radius: 62,
            step: 1,
            min: 0,
            max: 24,
            value: 6,
            theme: { fill: 0x30d158 },
        });
        const size = new Scrubber({ ticker, label: 'Size', value: 24, step: 1, min: 1, max: 200, perPixel: 0.5 });
        const opacity = new Scrubber({
            ticker,
            label: 'Opacity',
            value: 0.8,
            step: 0.05,
            min: 0,
            max: 1,
            perPixel: 0.005,
            format: (v) => `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`,
            theme: { fill: 0xbf5af2 },
        });
        const angle = new Scrubber({
            ticker,
            label: 'Angle',
            value: 0,
            step: 15,
            perPixel: 1,
            format: (v) => `${v.toFixed(1)}°`,
        });

        endless.position.set(110, 130);
        stepped.position.set(290, 130);
        size.position.set(420, 70);
        opacity.position.set(420, 125);
        angle.position.set(420, 180);
        // the endless dial drives the angle field
        endless.state.value.subscribe((v) => angle.state.value.set(Math.round(v * 10) / 10));
        stage.addChild(endless, stepped, size, opacity, angle);

        return undefined;
    },
});
```

Controls for numbers and time. `JogDial` and `Scrubber` are in `pixi-silk/ui/dial`, and
`MiniTimeline` is in `pixi-silk/ui/timeline`. For a rolling number readout, see the odometer on
[Interaction and motion](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/interaction-and-motion/#roll-a-number-with-an-odometer).

### Turn a value with a jog dial

```ts

const camera = { zoom: 1 };
const undo: number[] = [];
const zoom = new JogDial({
    radius: 64,
    turn: 2,
    step: 0.1,
    min: 0.1,
    max: 8,
    onChange: (v) => (camera.zoom = v),
    onCommit: (v) => undo.push(v),
});
```

Press anywhere on the dial and drag around its centre. The value follows the angle and keeps
adding up over full turns, so there is no seam at the top. Past the ring the dial turns slower the
further out the pointer is, which gives fine control without letting go.

A fast release keeps the dial spinning until `friction` stops it. With `min` and `max` the ring
becomes a gauge of the range, and past a limit the value stretches and springs back. A tap on the
right half steps up, on the left half down. `onChange` fires on every snapped change and
`onCommit` once the dial rests.

| Option | Default | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `turn` | the range, else 1 | Value change for one full turn near the ring. |
| `step` | `0` | Snap step. 0 turns snapping off. |
| `fine` | `1` | How fast precision grows with distance: the speed is `(radius / distance) ** fine`. 0 turns it off. |
| `rubber` | `0.12` | How far the value stretches past a limit, as a fraction of a turn. |
| `inertia` | `true` | Keep spinning after a flick. |
| `friction` | `3.5` | How quickly a flick slows down, about 1 / seconds. |

### Drag a number field

```ts

const size = new Scrubber({ label: 'Size', value: 24, step: 1, min: 1, max: 200, perPixel: 0.5 });
```

Drag the field sideways to change its value. Shift scrubs at a tenth of the speed
(`fineFactor`), and Ctrl or Cmd snaps to the step. A tap on either end steps down or up. With
`min` and `max` the field fills like a slider. `perPixel` defaults to a quarter of the step.

Both controls keep their value in `state.value`, a signal you can also write to move them.

### Scrub an animation timeline

Live demo `timeline` (source):

```ts
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { JogDial } from 'pixi-silk/ui/dial';
import { MiniTimeline } from 'pixi-silk/ui/timeline';
import { defineDemo, PALETTE } from './runtime';

// Scrub the ruler (past the ends it stretches), drag keys, Shift-click or box-select several,
// and move the green loop handles. The wheel zooms, sideways or with Shift it pans, and a
// double tap on the ruler zooms in or back out. Tap the dial to play or pause.
// The scene is drawn small and scaled up, so the ruler's 9 px labels read at 11 px.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Timeline and jog dial',
    size: [540, 184],
    touchAction: 'none',
    setup({ silk, stage, tick }) {
        const ticker = silk.app.ticker;
        const timeline = new MiniTimeline({
            ticker,
            width: 380,
            height: 70,
            duration: 120,
            loop: [24, 96],
            keys: [0, 12, 30, 44, 60, 72, 90, 108].map((time, i) => ({ id: `k${i}`, time })),
        });
        const ball = new SilkGraphics();
        const jog = new JogDial({ ticker, radius: 34, thickness: 10, turn: 24, readout: false });
        let last = 0;

        timeline.position.set(24, 104);
        jog.position.set(472, 139);
        // the dial scrubs the playhead, a tap on it plays or pauses
        jog.state.value.subscribe((v) => {
            timeline.state.time.set(Math.max(0, Math.min(120, timeline.state.time.value + v - last)));
            last = v;
        });
        jog.on('pointertap', () => timeline.toggle());
        timeline.state.playing.set(true);
        stage.addChild(ball, timeline, jog);
        tick(() => {
            // something to animate: a ball eased between the keys
            const time = timeline.state.time.value;
            const keys = timeline.keys.map((k) => k.time).sort((a, b) => a - b);
            const i = keys.findIndex((k) => k > time);
            const a = keys[Math.max(0, i - 1)] ?? 0;
            const b = keys[i] ?? 120;
            const u = b > a ? (time - a) / (b - a) : 0;
            const y = 80 - Math.sin(Math.PI * u) * 44;
            const x = 24 + (time / 120) * 380;

            ball.clear();
            ball.circle(x, 92, 12).fill({ color: 0x000000, alpha: 0.4, blur: 6 });
            ball.circle(x, y, 12).fill(PALETTE.blue);
        });

        return undefined;
    },
});
```

```ts

const timeline = new MiniTimeline({
    duration: 120,
    fps: 30,
    loop: [24, 96],
    keys: [
        { id: 'jump', time: 30 },
        { id: 'land', time: 44 },
    ],
    onKeysChange: (keys) => localStorage.setItem('keys', JSON.stringify(keys)),
});

timeline.state.time.subscribe((frame) => console.log('frame', frame));
timeline.state.playing.set(true);
```

Times are in frames. Scrub the ruler to move the playhead, which snaps to frames and stretches
past either end. Drag a key to move it, Shift + click to add keys to the selection, or drag on
empty track for a selection box. Selected keys move together and stop at the ends of the track.
Drag the green handles on the ruler to set the loop range, and playback wraps inside it.

The wheel zooms around the pointer and Shift + wheel pans. Pixi listens to the wheel passively,
so the page scrolls too unless the canvas fills it. Without a wheel, a double tap on the ruler
zooms in and another zooms back out. `zoomBy()`, `panBy()` and `showAll()` do the same from code.

| State | What it holds |
|---|---|
| `time` | Playhead time in frames. Writing it moves the playhead. |
| `playing` | Writing it starts or stops playback. |
| `selection` | Ids of the selected keys. |
| `range` | Loop range in frames, or null for the whole track. |
| `view` | The visible window in frames. |

| Option | Default | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `duration` | `120` | Length in frames. |
| `fps` | `30` | Playback speed. |
| `snap` | `1` | Keys and the playhead snap to multiples of this many frames. 0 turns it off. |
| `minSpan` | `8` | Fewest frames the view zooms in to. |

The timeline holds keys and a playhead, not animation curves. Use `state.time` to drive your own
tweens or a Spine track.

---

# Panels

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/panels/
Area: Editor kit

> Dockable panels drawn in Pixi with no DOM: a virtualised layer tree, a schema-driven property inspector with undo, and a minimap of the camera view.

> **NOTE**
> Experimental in 1.0: the API may change in a minor release.

Demo sources run in the docs demo runner, `defineDemo({ setup({ stage, params, tick }) })`. In your own app, `stage` is `app.stage` (or a container on it), `params` holds the values of the demo controls, and `tick(fn)` is `app.ticker.add`, with `t` and `dt` in seconds.

Live demo `editor-panels` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { bindHistoryKeys, History, recordGizmo } from 'pixi-silk/history';
import { bindGizmo, bindMeasure, bindOutline, Selection } from 'pixi-silk/selection';
import { watch } from 'pixi-silk/signal';
import { silkLabel } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { Camera } from 'pixi-silk/ui/camera';
import { TransformGizmo } from 'pixi-silk/ui/gizmo';
import { Inspector, type InspectorField } from 'pixi-silk/ui/inspector';
import { altDown } from 'pixi-silk/ui/keys';
import { LayerTree, pixiTreeAdapter } from 'pixi-silk/ui/layers';
import { Measure } from 'pixi-silk/ui/measure';
import { SelectionOutline } from 'pixi-silk/ui/outline';
import { PANEL_THEME, PANEL_THEME_LIGHT, Panel, type PanelTheme, panelHitTest } from 'pixi-silk/ui/panel';
import { BG, defineDemo } from './runtime';

/** A layer with a fill, a size and a corner radius the inspector can edit. */
class Shape extends Container {
    private readonly g = new SilkGraphics();
    private _fill: number;
    private _w: number;
    private _h: number;
    private _radius: number;
    readonly round: boolean;

    constructor(label: string, w: number, h: number, fill: number, radius = 0, round = false) {
        super({ label });
        this._w = w;
        this._h = h;
        this._fill = fill;
        this._radius = radius;
        this.round = round;
        this.addChild(this.g);
        this.redraw();
    }

    get fill(): number {
        return this._fill;
    }
    set fill(v: number) {
        this._fill = v;
        this.redraw();
    }
    get w(): number {
        return this._w;
    }
    set w(v: number) {
        this._w = Math.max(1, v);
        this.redraw();
    }
    get h(): number {
        return this._h;
    }
    set h(v: number) {
        this._h = Math.max(1, v);
        this.redraw();
    }
    get radius(): number {
        return this._radius;
    }
    set radius(v: number) {
        this._radius = Math.max(0, v);
        this.redraw();
    }

    private redraw(): void {
        const g = this.g.clear();

        if (this.round) g.ellipse(this._w / 2, this._h / 2, this._w / 2, this._h / 2).fill(this._fill);
        else
            g.roundRect(0, 0, this._w, this._h, Math.min(this._radius, this._w / 2, this._h / 2), 0.6).fill(this._fill);
    }
}

// The inspector rows for a layer. Keys are paths on the selected objects.
const schema: InspectorField[] = [
    {
        type: 'section',
        label: 'Layer',
        fields: [
            { type: 'text', key: 'label', label: 'Name' },
            { type: 'toggle', key: 'visible', label: 'Visible' },
            { type: 'number', key: 'alpha', label: 'Opacity', min: 0, max: 1, step: 0.01, perPixel: 0.005 },
            {
                type: 'choice',
                key: 'blendMode',
                label: 'Blend',
                options: [
                    { value: 'inherit', label: 'Pass' },
                    { value: 'normal', label: 'Normal' },
                    { value: 'add', label: 'Add' },
                ],
            },
        ],
    },
    {
        type: 'section',
        label: 'Transform',
        fields: [
            { type: 'vector', keys: ['position.x', 'position.y'], label: 'Position' },
            { type: 'vector', keys: ['w', 'h'], label: 'Size', labels: ['W', 'H'], min: 1, lockAspect: true },
            { type: 'number', key: 'angle', label: 'Rotation', step: 1, unit: 'deg' },
        ],
    },
    {
        type: 'section',
        label: 'Fill',
        fields: [
            { type: 'color', key: 'fill', label: 'Colour' },
            { type: 'number', key: 'radius', label: 'Radius', min: 0, step: 1, unit: 'px' },
        ],
    },
];

// A small editor drawn entirely on the canvas: layers on the left, the inspector on the right and
// the scene in the middle. Pick a layer in the list or on the canvas, drag rows to reorder them,
// scrub a number, or open the colour swatch. Cmd or ctrl + Z undoes every step. Drag a panel by
// its title to float it, and drop it near an edge to dock it. On a narrow canvas both panels start
// folded: click a chevron to open one.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Editor panels',
    size: [720, 620],
    touchAction: 'none',
    ownsCamera: true,
    controls: {
        theme: { type: 'select', label: 'theme', options: ['dark', 'light'], value: 'dark' },
    },
    setup({ silk, params, tick }) {
        const app = silk.app;
        const world = new Container({ label: 'Page' });
        const overlay = new Container();
        const panelLayer = new Container();
        const selection = new Selection<Container>();
        const history = new History();
        const label = silkLabel();
        const hover = new SelectionOutline({ ticker: app.ticker });
        const outline = new SelectionOutline({ ticker: app.ticker });
        const measure = new Measure({ space: world, ticker: app.ticker, label });
        const gizmo = new TransformGizmo({ ticker: app.ticker, onChange: () => synced() });
        const panels: Panel[] = [];
        const camera = new Camera({ world, element: app.canvas, ignore: panelHitTest(() => panels) });
        const viewport = { width: camera.state.width, height: camera.state.height };

        gizmo.state.mode.set('move');
        overlay.addChild(hover, outline, gizmo, measure);
        app.stage.addChild(world, overlay, panelLayer);
        app.stage.eventMode = 'static';
        app.stage.hitArea = app.screen;
        app.stage.on('pointerdown', (e) => e.target === app.stage && !e.shiftKey && selection.clear());

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the scene
        const layer = <T extends Container>(item: T, parent: Container, x: number, y: number): T => {
            item.position.set(x, y);
            item.eventMode = 'static';
            item.cursor = 'pointer';
            item.on('pointerover', () => selection.state.hovered.set(item));
            item.on('pointerout', () => {
                if (selection.state.hovered.value === item) selection.state.hovered.set(null);
            });
            item.on('pointerdown', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                selection.pick(item, e.shiftKey ? 'toggle' : 'replace');
            });
            parent.addChild(item);

            return item;
        };
        const group = (name: string, parent: Container, x = 0, y = 0) => {
            const g = new Container({ label: name });

            g.position.set(x, y);
            parent.addChild(g);

            return g;
        };

        const home = group('Home', world);

        layer(new Shape('Background', 390, 620, 0xf4f4f6), home, 0, 0);
        layer(new Shape('Hero', 342, 180, 0x5e5ce6, 24), home, 24, 24);
        const cards = group('Cards', home, 24, 228);

        layer(new Shape('Card A', 160, 120, 0xffffff, 18), cards, 0, 0);
        layer(new Shape('Card B', 160, 120, 0xffffff, 18), cards, 182, 0);
        const list = group('List', home, 24, 372);

        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            const row = group(`Row ${i + 1}`, list, 0, i * 52);

            layer(new Shape('Row background', 342, 44, 0xffffff, 12), row, 0, 0);
            layer(new Shape('Avatar', 28, 28, [0x30d158, 0xff9f0a, 0xff375f][i], 0, true), row, 10, 8);
            layer(new Shape('Title', 140, 10, 0xd1d1d6, 5), row, 50, 17);
        }
        layer(new Shape('Tab bar', 342, 52, 0x1c1c1e, 26), home, 24, 548);
        const profile = group('Profile', world, 470, 0);

        layer(new Shape('Background', 390, 620, 0xf4f4f6), profile, 0, 0);
        layer(new Shape('Cover', 390, 200, 0xbf5af2), profile, 0, 0);
        layer(new Shape('Photo', 96, 96, 0xffd60a, 0, true), profile, 147, 152);
        layer(new Shape('Bio', 300, 80, 0xffffff, 16), profile, 45, 280);

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the panels
        let ui: ReturnType<typeof buildPanels> | null = null;
        const synced = () => ui?.inspector.refresh();
        // panel widths for the canvas the demo got: a phone gets two folded title bars
        const narrow = silk.width < 560;
        const sizes = narrow ? { layers: 140, inspector: 176 } : { layers: 196, inspector: 244 };
        // the light theme builds the panels again with the other tokens
        const buildPanels = (theme: Partial<PanelTheme>) => {
            const layersPanel = new Panel({
                title: 'Layers',
                dock: narrow ? 'top-left' : 'left',
                width: sizes.layers,
                minWidth: sizes.layers,
                height: 360,
                collapsed: narrow,
                viewport,
                scroll: false,
                theme,
            });
            const inspectorPanel = new Panel({
                title: 'Inspector',
                dock: narrow ? 'top-right' : 'right',
                width: sizes.inspector,
                minWidth: sizes.inspector,
                height: 420,
                collapsed: narrow,
                viewport,
                theme,
            });
            const tree = new LayerTree({
                adapter: pixiTreeAdapter({ leaf: (c) => c instanceof Shape }),
                root: world,
                selection,
                history,
                element: app.canvas,
                theme,
            });
            const inspector = new Inspector({ schema, history, element: app.canvas, popoverLayer: panelLayer, theme });

            layersPanel.content.add(tree, { grow: 1, onSize: (w, h) => tree.resize(w, h) });
            inspectorPanel.content.add(inspector);
            panels.length = 0;
            panels.push(layersPanel, inspectorPanel);
            panelLayer.addChild(...panels);
            const stops = [
                watch(selection.state.items, (items) => {
                    inspector.bind(items);
                    inspectorPanel.title =
                        items.length > 1 ? `${items.length} layers` : (items[0]?.label ?? 'Inspector');
                }),
                history.onChange(() => {
                    tree.refresh();
                    synced();
                }),
            ];

            return {
                inspector,
                destroy: () => {
                    for (const stop of stops) stop();
                    for (const p of panels) p.destroy({ children: true });
                },
            };
        };

        ui = buildPanels(PANEL_THEME);
        const redlines = () => altDown();
        const measured = bindMeasure(selection, measure, { between: redlines });
        const syncHover = () => {
            const over = selection.state.hovered.value;

            hover.hover(!over || selection.has(over) ? null : over);
        };
        // everything reads the one selection, and every change goes through the one history
        const stops = [
            bindOutline(selection, outline),
            bindGizmo(selection, gizmo),
            measured.stop,
            recordGizmo(gizmo, history),
            bindHistoryKeys(history),
            watch(selection.state.hovered, syncHover),
            watch(selection.state.items, syncHover),
        ];
        let theme = params.theme;

        tick(() => {
            if (params.theme === theme) return;
            theme = params.theme;
            app.renderer.background.color = theme === 'light' ? 0xe4e4ea : BG;
            ui?.destroy();
            ui = buildPanels(theme === 'light' ? PANEL_THEME_LIGHT : PANEL_THEME);
        });

        selection.pick(cards.children[0] as Container);
        // frame the first artboard in the space the panels leave free, again when the canvas gets its size
        const frame = () =>
            camera.fit(
                home,
                narrow
                    ? { left: 24, right: 24, top: 64, bottom: 24 }
                    : { left: sizes.layers + 20, right: sizes.inspector + 20, top: 24, bottom: 24 },
                false,
            );
        let framed = camera.state.width.value > 0;
        const stopFrame = camera.state.width.subscribe((w) => {
            if (framed || w <= 0) return;
            framed = true;
            frame();
        });

        stops.push(stopFrame);
        frame();

        return () => {
            for (const stop of stops) stop();
            ui?.destroy();
            camera.destroy();
        };
    },
});
```

The panels around an editor canvas, drawn as Pixi containers. They take the same pointer events
as the scene, lay out with the [flex layout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/layout/) and draw text with
[SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/text/). You keep one event system and one style instead of DOM over a canvas.

| Entry point | What it holds |
| --- | --- |
| `pixi-silk/ui/panel` | `Panel`, `ScrollView`, the themes, `dockRect` and `panelHitTest` |
| `pixi-silk/ui/layers` | `LayerTree`, `pixiTreeAdapter` and the tree functions |
| `pixi-silk/ui/inspector` | `Inspector`, its fields, `bindObjects` and `bindSignals` |
| `pixi-silk/ui/minimap` | `Minimap` and its mapping functions |

### Dock a panel

Live demo `panel-dock` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { PANEL_THEME, PANEL_THEME_LIGHT, Panel } from 'pixi-silk/ui/panel';
import { BG, defineDemo } from './runtime';

// Drag a panel by its title bar. Drop it near an edge or a corner to dock it there, drag it off to
// float it again. Drag an edge to resize it, click the chevron to fold it, and scroll its list.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Docking panels',
    size: [720, 460],
    touchAction: 'none',
    controls: {
        theme: { type: 'select', label: 'theme', options: ['dark', 'light'], value: 'dark' },
    },
    setup({ silk, stage, width, height, params, tick }) {
        const viewport = { width, height };
        const canvas = silk.app.canvas;
        // the panels scroll with the wheel: keep the page still while the pointer is over the demo
        const still = (e: WheelEvent) => e.preventDefault();

        canvas.addEventListener('wheel', still, { passive: false });
        const backdrop = new SilkGraphics();

        stage.addChild(backdrop);
        let panels: Panel[] = [];
        const build = (light: boolean) => {
            for (const p of panels) p.destroy({ children: true });
            const theme = light ? PANEL_THEME_LIGHT : PANEL_THEME;

            backdrop
                .clear()
                .rect(0, 0, width, height)
                .fill(light ? 0xe6e6eb : BG);
            const colours = new Panel({ title: 'Colours', x: 200, y: 60, width: 240, height: 320, viewport, theme });
            const notes = new Panel({ title: 'Notes', dock: 'right', width: 220, viewport, theme });

            // forty swatch rows, laid out by the panel's flex column and clipped by its body
            for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
                const row = new Container();
                const hue = (i * 37) % 360;
                const chip = new SilkGraphics().roundRect(0, 0, 22, 22, 6, 0.6).fill(`hsl(${hue} 70% 58%)`);
                const name = new SilkText({
                    text: `Swatch ${i + 1}`,
                    fontFamily: theme.fontFamily,
                    fontSize: 12,
                    fontWeight: 500,
                    fill: theme.text,
                });
                const code = new SilkText({
                    text: `${hue} deg`,
                    fontFamily: theme.fontFamily,
                    fontSize: 11,
                    fill: theme.textDim,
                    anchor: { x: 1, y: 0 },
                });

                name.position.set(32, 4);
                code.position.set(216, 5);
                row.addChild(chip, name, code);
                colours.content.add(row);
            }
            const text = new SilkText({
                text: 'Panels are Pixi containers. They pan, zoom and stay sharp like the rest of the scene, and need no DOM on top of the canvas.',
                fontFamily: theme.fontFamily,
                fontSize: 12,
                lineHeight: 18,
                fill: theme.textDim,
                wordWrap: true,
                wordWrapWidth: 190,
            });

            notes.content.add(text);
            panels = [colours, notes];
            stage.addChild(...panels);
        };
        let light = params.theme === 'light';

        build(light);
        tick(() => {
            if ((params.theme === 'light') === light) return;
            light = params.theme === 'light';
            build(light);
        });

        return () => {
            canvas.removeEventListener('wheel', still);
        };
    },
});
```

```ts

declare const camera: Camera, stage: Container, rows: Container[];

const panel = new Panel({ title: 'Library', dock: 'left', width: 260, viewport: camera.state });

for (const row of rows) panel.content.add(row);
stage.addChild(panel);
```

A panel has a title bar, a body and edges to resize. While a dragged panel nears a screen edge or
corner, a preview shows where it docks. Left and right panels span the screen height, top and
bottom ones its width. `viewport` is the screen size, as numbers or signals. The camera's state
works.

The body, `panel.content`, is a flex column. It scrolls when it is taller than the panel, with a
scrollbar that fades in while it moves. A widget that scrolls itself, like the layer tree, wants
`scroll: false`, and the body is then a flex container as tall as the panel.

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `dock` | floating | An edge or a corner, such as `'left'` or `'top-right'`. Needs `viewport`. |
| `width`, `height` | `260`, `360` | Size when floating. A docked panel spans its edge. |
| `margin` | `8` | Gap to the screen edge when docked. |
| `insets` | none | Space kept free along each edge, for a toolbar. |
| `collapsible`, `collapsed` | `true`, `false` | The chevron folds the body away. |
| `draggable`, `resizable`, `dockable` | `true` | Move, resize and dock by dragging. |
| `scroll` | `true` | Scroll `content`, or fill the body with it. |
| `theme` | dark | `PANEL_THEME_LIGHT`, or tokens over the dark theme. |

`panel.state.collapsed` and `panel.state.dock` are signals. Write them to fold or dock from code.
`panel.actions` is a row in the title bar for your own buttons.

The [camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/canvas-and-rulers/) listens to the wheel on the whole canvas. Pass
`ignore: panelHitTest(panels)` to it, and wheels and pan drags over a panel stay with the panel.

### List layers

Live demo `layers-tree` (source):

```ts
import { History } from 'pixi-silk/history';
import { Selection } from 'pixi-silk/selection';
import { watch } from 'pixi-silk/signal';
import { SilkText } from 'pixi-silk/text';
import { type LayerIcon, LayerTree, type TreeAdapter } from 'pixi-silk/ui/layers';
import { PANEL_THEME, Panel } from 'pixi-silk/ui/panel';
import { defineDemo, INK } from './runtime';

/** The app's own model: plain objects, no Pixi in them. */
interface Item {
    name: string;
    kind: 'frame' | 'group' | 'shape' | 'text';
    children: Item[];
    parent: Item | null;
    hidden: boolean;
    locked: boolean;
}

const item = (name: string, kind: Item['kind'], parent: Item | null): Item => {
    const it: Item = { name, kind, children: [], parent, hidden: false, locked: false };

    parent?.children.push(it);

    return it;
};

// Two thousand layers in a plain object model. Only the rows in view are drawn. Click, Shift +
// click and cmd or ctrl + click select. Drag rows to reorder them, or drop them on a group to move
// them inside. Double click a name to rename it. Arrow keys move the selection.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Layer tree',
    size: [720, 460],
    touchAction: 'none',
    setup({ silk, stage }) {
        const canvas = silk.app.canvas;
        const still = (e: WheelEvent) => e.preventDefault();

        canvas.addEventListener('wheel', still, { passive: false });
        const root = item('Document', 'frame', null);
        let count = 0;

        for (let f = 0; f < 40; f++) {
            const frame = item(`Screen ${f + 1}`, 'frame', root);

            for (let g = 0; g < 5; g++) {
                const group = item(`Section ${g + 1}`, 'group', frame);

                for (let s = 0; s < 9; s++)
                    item(s % 3 === 0 ? `Label ${s + 1}` : `Shape ${s + 1}`, s % 3 ? 'shape' : 'text', group);
                count += 10;
            }
            count++;
        }
        const adapter: TreeAdapter<Item> = {
            getChildren: (n) => n.children,
            getName: (n) => n.name,
            getParent: (n) => n.parent,
            setName: (n, name) => {
                n.name = name;
            },
            getIcon: (n): LayerIcon => n.kind,
            isVisible: (n) => !n.hidden,
            setVisible: (n, v) => {
                n.hidden = !v;
            },
            isLocked: (n) => n.locked,
            setLocked: (n, v) => {
                n.locked = v;
            },
            canHaveChildren: (n) => n.kind === 'frame' || n.kind === 'group',
            move: (n, parent, index) => {
                n.parent?.children.splice(n.parent.children.indexOf(n), 1);
                parent.children.splice(index, 0, n);
                n.parent = parent;
            },
        };
        const selection = new Selection<Item>();
        const history = new History();
        const panel = new Panel({ title: 'Layers', x: 24, y: 24, width: 300, height: 412, scroll: false });
        const tree = new LayerTree({ adapter, root, selection, history, element: canvas });
        const info = new SilkText({
            fontFamily: PANEL_THEME.fontFamily,
            fontSize: 13,
            lineHeight: 20,
            fill: INK.muted,
            wordWrap: true,
            wordWrapWidth: 340,
        });
        const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            const mod = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;

            if (!mod || e.code !== 'KeyZ') return;
            e.preventDefault();
            if (e.shiftKey) history.redo();
            else history.undo();
        };

        addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        tree.expand(root.children[0], true);
        panel.content.add(tree, { grow: 1, onSize: (w, h) => tree.resize(w, h) });
        info.position.set(352, 40);
        stage.addChild(panel, info);
        const show = () => {
            const picked = selection.items.map((n) => n.name);

            info.text = [
                `${count} layers, ${tree.rows.length} rows open`,
                '',
                picked.length
                    ? `Selected: ${picked.slice(0, 6).join(', ')}${picked.length > 6 ? ' and more' : ''}`
                    : 'Nothing selected',
                '',
                history.state.undoLabel.value ? `Undo: ${history.state.undoLabel.value}` : 'Nothing to undo',
            ].join('\n');
        };
        const stops = [watch(selection.state.items, show), watch(tree.state.rows, show), history.onChange(show)];

        return () => {
            for (const stop of stops) stop();
            removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
            canvas.removeEventListener('wheel', still);
        };
    },
});
```

```ts

declare const world: Container, panel: Panel;

const selection = new Selection<Container>();
const tree = new LayerTree({ adapter: pixiTreeAdapter(), root: world, selection, history: new History() });

panel.content.add(tree, { grow: 1, onSize: (w, h) => tree.resize(w, h) });
```

`LayerTree` reads your data through a `TreeAdapter` and keeps no copy of it, so call `refresh()`
after the data changes elsewhere. Only the rows in view exist, so two thousand layers scroll like
twenty. Rows are listed front to back, so the adapter's first child is the top row.

The rows follow a [`Selection`](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/gizmos-and-selection/) both ways. A pick on the canvas opens
its groups and scrolls the row into view. Hovering a row sets `selection.state.hovered`, so the
canvas can outline it. Drag rows between others to reorder them, or onto a group to move them
inside. On touch, hold a row to pick it up. Every rename, toggle and move is one step in `history`.

`pixiTreeAdapter()` maps containers to rows. The name is `label`, the eye sets `visible`, and the
lock turns pointer events off. For your own model, write the adapter:

```ts

interface Item {
    name: string;
    children: Item[];
    parent: Item | null;
}

declare const doc: Item;

const adapter: TreeAdapter<Item> = {
    getChildren: (n) => n.children,
    getName: (n) => n.name,
    getParent: (n) => n.parent,
    setName: (n, name) => (n.name = name),
    move: (n, parent, index) => {
        n.parent?.children.splice(n.parent.children.indexOf(n), 1);
        parent.children.splice(index, 0, n);
        n.parent = parent;
    },
};

const tree = new LayerTree({ adapter, root: doc });
```

The tree logic has no Pixi in it and is exported on its own: `flattenTree`, `visibleRange`,
`dropTarget`, `applyDrop` and `undoMoves`.

### Edit properties

Live demo `inspector-schema` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { bindHistoryKeys, History } from 'pixi-silk/history';
import { bindOutline, Selection } from 'pixi-silk/selection';
import { watch } from 'pixi-silk/signal';
import { Inspector, type InspectorField } from 'pixi-silk/ui/inspector';
import { SelectionOutline } from 'pixi-silk/ui/outline';
import { Panel } from 'pixi-silk/ui/panel';
import { defineDemo, PALETTE } from './runtime';

/** A card with a fill and a corner radius, so the inspector has something of its own to edit. */
class Card extends Container {
    private readonly g = new SilkGraphics();
    private _fill: number;
    private _radius = 18;
    shadow = true;

    constructor(label: string, fill: number) {
        super({ label });
        this._fill = fill;
        this.addChild(this.g);
        this.redraw();
    }

    get fill(): number {
        return this._fill;
    }
    set fill(v: number) {
        this._fill = v;
        this.redraw();
    }
    get radius(): number {
        return this._radius;
    }
    set radius(v: number) {
        this._radius = v;
        this.redraw();
    }
    get glow(): boolean {
        return this.shadow;
    }
    set glow(v: boolean) {
        this.shadow = v;
        this.redraw();
    }

    redraw(): void {
        const g = this.g.clear();

        if (this.shadow)
            g.roundRect(0, 8, 120, 120, this._radius, 0.6).fill({ color: this._fill, alpha: 0.45, blur: 7 });
        g.roundRect(0, 0, 120, 120, this._radius, 0.6).fill(this._fill);
    }
}

const schema: InspectorField[] = [
    { type: 'text', key: 'label', label: 'Name' },
    { type: 'vector', keys: ['position.x', 'position.y'], label: 'Position' },
    { type: 'vector', keys: ['scale.x', 'scale.y'], label: 'Scale', labels: ['X', 'Y'], step: 0.01, lockAspect: true },
    { type: 'number', key: 'angle', label: 'Rotation', unit: 'deg' },
    { type: 'number', key: 'alpha', label: 'Opacity', min: 0, max: 1, step: 0.01, perPixel: 0.005 },
    {
        type: 'section',
        label: 'Style',
        fields: [
            { type: 'color', key: 'fill', label: 'Fill' },
            { type: 'number', key: 'radius', label: 'Radius', min: 0, max: 60, unit: 'px' },
            { type: 'toggle', key: 'glow', label: 'Glow' },
            {
                type: 'choice',
                key: 'blendMode',
                label: 'Blend',
                options: [
                    { value: 'inherit', label: 'Pass' },
                    { value: 'normal', label: 'Normal' },
                    { value: 'add', label: 'Add' },
                ],
            },
        ],
    },
];

// Click a card, or Shift + click to edit several: values that differ show as Mixed, and a scrub
// moves each from its own start. Drag a number sideways, or click it to type. Open the swatch for
// the colour picker. Cmd or ctrl + Z undoes each drag as one step.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Inspector from a schema',
    size: [720, 480],
    touchAction: 'none',
    setup({ silk, stage }) {
        const canvas = silk.app.canvas;
        const still = (e: WheelEvent) => e.preventDefault();

        canvas.addEventListener('wheel', still, { passive: false });
        const selection = new Selection<Container>();
        const history = new History();
        const scene = new Container();
        const outline = new SelectionOutline({ ticker: silk.app.ticker });
        const panel = new Panel({ title: 'Inspector', x: 412, y: 24, width: 284, height: 432 });
        const inspector = new Inspector({ schema, history, element: canvas, popoverLayer: stage });
        const cards = [new Card('Blue', PALETTE.blue), new Card('Pink', PALETTE.pink), new Card('Mint', PALETTE.green)];

        cards.forEach((card, i) => {
            card.position.set(48 + (i % 2) * 180, 56 + Math.floor(i / 2) * 210 + (i % 2) * 40);
            card.eventMode = 'static';
            card.cursor = 'pointer';
            card.on('pointerdown', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                selection.pick(card, e.shiftKey ? 'toggle' : 'replace');
            });
            scene.addChild(card);
        });
        stage.eventMode = 'static';
        stage.hitArea = { contains: () => true };
        stage.on('pointerdown', (e) => {
            if (e.target === stage) selection.clear();
        });
        panel.content.add(inspector);
        stage.addChild(scene, outline, panel);
        const stops = [
            bindOutline(selection, outline),
            watch(selection.state.items, (items) => {
                inspector.bind(items);
                panel.title = items.length > 1 ? `${items.length} cards` : (items[0]?.label ?? 'Inspector');
            }),
            history.onChange(() => inspector.refresh()),
            bindHistoryKeys(history),
        ];

        selection.replace([cards[0], cards[1]]);

        return () => {
            for (const stop of stops) stop();
            canvas.removeEventListener('wheel', still);
        };
    },
});
```

```ts

declare const selection: Selection<Container>, panel: Panel;

const inspector = new Inspector({
    schema: [
        { type: 'text', key: 'label', label: 'Name' },
        { type: 'vector', keys: ['position.x', 'position.y'], label: 'Position' },
        { type: 'number', key: 'angle', label: 'Rotation', unit: 'deg' },
        {
            type: 'section',
            label: 'Layer',
            fields: [
                { type: 'number', key: 'alpha', label: 'Opacity', min: 0, max: 1, step: 0.01 },
                { type: 'toggle', key: 'visible', label: 'Visible' },
                { type: 'color', key: 'tint', label: 'Tint' },
            ],
        },
    ],
    history: new History(),
});

selection.state.items.subscribe((items) => inspector.bind(items));
panel.content.add(inspector);
```

Each schema entry names a path on the edited objects, such as `position.x`, and a field type.

| Type | Field |
| --- | --- |
| `number` | Drag sideways to scrub, Shift for faster, Alt for finer. Click to type. Takes `min`, `max`, `step` and `unit`. |
| `text` | A one-line [text editor](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/editing-text/). |
| `toggle` | A switch. |
| `choice` | Segmented options. |
| `color` | A swatch and its hex code. A click opens a [colour picker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/docs/color-pickers/). |
| `vector` | Two numbers in one row. `lockAspect` keeps their ratio. |
| `section` | A titled group that folds. |

With several objects bound, values that differ show as Mixed. A scrub moves each value from its
own start, and a typed value sets them all. Every edit is one step in `history`.

`bind()` also takes an `InspectorBinding` with `read` and `write`, for a store of your own.
`bindSignals()` edits signals, for a settings panel. The inspector does not watch the objects, so
call `refresh()` when they change elsewhere, such as during a gizmo drag.

### Show a minimap

Live demo `minimap` (source):

```ts
import { Container } from 'pixi.js';
import { SilkGraphics } from 'pixi-silk';
import { Camera } from 'pixi-silk/ui/camera';
import { Minimap } from 'pixi-silk/ui/minimap';
import { panelHitTest } from 'pixi-silk/ui/panel';
import { defineDemo, SERIES } from './runtime';

// Scroll to pan and pinch or ctrl + scroll to zoom. The minimap follows the view. Drag its frame
// to pan, click anywhere on it to jump there, and scroll over it to zoom.
export default defineDemo({
    title: 'Minimap',
    size: [720, 460],
    touchAction: 'none',
    ownsCamera: true,
    controls: {
        mode: { type: 'select', label: 'thumbnail', options: ['bounds', 'texture'], value: 'bounds' },
    },
    setup({ silk, params, tick }) {
        const app = silk.app;
        const world = new Container();
        const maps: Container[] = [];
        const camera = new Camera({ world, element: app.canvas, ignore: panelHitTest(() => maps) });

        app.stage.addChild(world);
        // a board of cards in rows, some wide, some tall
        const colours = SERIES;

        for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
            const w = 120 + ((i * 53) % 140);
            const h = 80 + ((i * 31) % 120);
            const g = new SilkGraphics().roundRect(0, 0, w, h, 16, 0.6).fill(colours[i % colours.length]);

            g.position.set((i % 10) * 300 + ((i * 17) % 40), Math.floor(i / 10) * 260 + ((i * 29) % 50));
            world.addChild(g);
        }
        let map: Minimap | null = null;
        const place = () =>
            map?.position.set(app.screen.width - map.mapWidth - 16, app.screen.height - map.mapHeight - 16);
        const build = (mode: 'bounds' | 'texture') => {
            map?.destroy();
            // a third of a narrow canvas at most, so the board stays in view on a phone
            const width = Math.min(200, Math.round(app.screen.width * 0.34));

            map = new Minimap({ camera, width, height: Math.round(width * 0.62), mode, renderer: app.renderer });
            maps[0] = map;
            app.stage.addChild(map);
            place();
        };
        let mode = params.mode;

        build(mode as 'bounds' | 'texture');
        app.renderer.on('resize', place);
        tick(() => {
            if (params.mode === mode) return;
            mode = params.mode;
            build(mode as 'bounds' | 'texture');
        });
        camera.fit({ x: 0, y: 0, width: 1200, height: 700 }, 40, false);

        return () => {
            app.renderer.off('resize', place);
            camera.destroy();
        };
    },
});
```

```ts

declare const camera: Camera;

const map = new Minimap({ camera, width: 220, height: 140 });

app.stage.addChild(map);
```

The minimap frames the content and the view together. The default `mode: 'bounds'` draws the
outline of each top-level object. `mode: 'texture'` renders a real thumbnail every `interval`
seconds (0.5 by default) and needs the `renderer`. Call `refresh()` after the content moves.

---

# API reference

One overview per entry point, with every export and its summary. Each link opens the Markdown page of that export.

## `pixi-silk/history`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/

History (undo and redo), recordSignals, recordGizmo, bindHistoryKeys.

```ts
import { History, bindHistoryKeys, recordGizmo, recordSignals } from 'pixi-silk/history';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [History](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/classes/History.md) | The default undo stack: steps in memory, transactions, merging and a depth limit. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Command](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/Command.md) | One undoable change. |
| [HistoryKeysOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/HistoryKeysOptions.md) | Options of [bindHistoryKeys](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/bindHistoryKeys.md). |
| [HistoryLike](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/HistoryLike.md) | What undoable widgets need from a history. Implement it to plug in your own store: `record` gets changes that are already applied, `begin` and `end` bracket a gesture such as a drag. |
| [HistoryOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/HistoryOptions.md) | Options of [History](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/classes/History.md). |
| [HistoryState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/HistoryState.md) | The signals of a [History](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/classes/History.md). |
| [HistoryStep](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/HistoryStep.md) | One entry of the stack: a label and the commands it undoes together. |
| [RecordableGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/RecordableGizmo.md) | What [recordGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/recordGizmo.md) needs from a gizmo, such as `TransformGizmo`. |
| [RecordGizmoOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/RecordGizmoOptions.md) | Options of [recordGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/recordGizmo.md). |
| [RecordSignalsOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/RecordSignalsOptions.md) | Options of [recordSignals](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/recordSignals.md). |
| [SignalRecorder](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/SignalRecorder.md) | What [recordSignals](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/recordSignals.md) returns. |
| [TransformSnapshot](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/TransformSnapshot.md) | Position, rotation and scale, the way a transform gizmo reads them. |
| [TransformTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/interfaces/TransformTarget.md) | Something with a position, rotation and scale, such as a Pixi `Container`. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [bindHistoryKeys](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/bindHistoryKeys.md) | Binds cmd or ctrl + Z to undo, and shift + cmd or ctrl + Z and ctrl + Y to redo. Keys typed into inputs, text areas and editable elements are left alone. Returns the unbind function. |
| [recordGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/recordGizmo.md) | Records every drag of a transform gizmo as one undoable step. The gizmo knows nothing about history: this watches its `dragging` signal. Returns the function that stops recording. |
| [recordSignals](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/history/functions/recordSignals.md) | Records every change of `signals` into `history`, so undo works without writing commands. Changes made in the same synchronous run (one handler, one `batch`) become one step. Inside a transaction everything merges into the transaction's step. |

## `pixi-silk/layout`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/

LayoutNode: a headless flex layout engine with no Pixi in it.

```ts
import { LayoutNode } from 'pixi-silk/layout';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [LayoutNode](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/classes/LayoutNode.md) | A box in a layout tree. Give it a style, children or a `measure` function, then call [LayoutNode.calculate](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/classes/LayoutNode.md) on the root. Each node's [LayoutNode.layout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/classes/LayoutNode.md) holds its box. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ComputedLayout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/interfaces/ComputedLayout.md) | The box a layout pass computed for a node, relative to its parent's top-left corner. |
| [LayoutStyle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/interfaces/LayoutStyle.md) | The style of a layout node. Every property is optional and defaults to the CSS initial value. |
| [Size](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/interfaces/Size.md) | A size in pixels. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [AlignContent](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/AlignContent.md) | Cross axis distribution of the lines of a wrapping container. |
| [AlignItems](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/AlignItems.md) | Cross axis alignment of items in their line. `baseline` lines up first text baselines in a row. |
| [Edges](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/Edges.md) | Box edges, as in CSS: one value for all sides, `[vertical, horizontal]`, `[top, horizontal, bottom]` or `[top, right, bottom, left]`. |
| [FlexDirection](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/FlexDirection.md) | Main axis of a container. `row` lays children out left to right, `column` top to bottom. |
| [JustifyContent](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/JustifyContent.md) | Main axis distribution of the free space in a line. |
| [Length](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/Length.md) | A length: pixels, a percent of the containing box (`'50%'`) or `'auto'`. |
| [MeasureFunction](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/layout/types/MeasureFunction.md) | Measures leaf content (text, a sprite) for the space offered. Either argument is `Infinity` when that axis is unconstrained. Return the content size without padding. `baseline` is the distance from the top to the first text baseline, for `alignItems: 'baseline'`. |

## `pixi-silk/path`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/

Bezier path model: BezierPath, drawPath, parseSvgPath, pathToSvg and edit operations.

```ts
import { anchorsInRect, clonePath, cubicPoint, drawPath } from 'pixi-silk/path';
```

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [BezierPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/interfaces/BezierPath.md) | A path: anchors in order. A closed path also joins the last anchor back to the first. |
| [PathAnchor](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/interfaces/PathAnchor.md) | One point of a path. `in` and `out` are handle offsets from the anchor, not positions. |
| [PathHitPoint](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/interfaces/PathHitPoint.md) | The closest point of a path to a point, as [nearestOnPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/nearestOnPath.md) finds it. |
| [PathSink](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/interfaces/PathSink.md) | What [drawPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/drawPath.md) draws into: `SilkGraphics`, Pixi `Graphics` or a canvas 2D context. |
| [Vec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/interfaces/Vec.md) | A point or an offset. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [AnchorType](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/types/AnchorType.md) | How an anchor's two handles move together. - `corner`: each handle moves on its own. Without handles it is a sharp corner. - `smooth`: the handles stay on one line, each keeps its own length. - `mirrored`: the handles stay on one line and have the same length. |
| [Cubic](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/types/Cubic.md) | A cubic bezier segment: start, first control point, second control point, end. |
| [HandleSide](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/types/HandleSide.md) | Which handle of an anchor. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [anchorsInRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/anchorsInRect.md) | Indices of the anchors inside a rectangle, edges included, for marquee selection. A negative width or height works. Handles are ignored. |
| [clonePath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/clonePath.md) | A deep copy of `path`. |
| [cubicPoint](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/cubicPoint.md) | Point at `t` (0 to 1) along a cubic. |
| [drawPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/drawPath.md) | Draws `path` into `g` with moveTo, lineTo, bezierCurveTo and closePath, then returns `g` for `.fill()` and `.stroke()`. A `SilkGraphics` fills it even-odd. |
| [inferAnchorType](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/inferAnchorType.md) | The type an anchor's handles imply: `mirrored`, `smooth` or `corner`. |
| [insertAnchor](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/insertAnchor.md) | Inserts an anchor on segment `segment` at `t` without changing the shape: the segment is split exactly (de Casteljau) and the neighbours' handles shrink to match. Returns the new path and the new anchor's index. |
| [isStraight](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/isStraight.md) | True when segment `i` has no handles: a straight line. |
| [mapPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/mapPath.md) | Maps every anchor and handle through `fn`, such as a matrix's `apply`. Exact for affine maps. |
| [moveAnchors](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/moveAnchors.md) | Moves the anchors at `indices` by (dx, dy). Their handles move with them. |
| [nearestOnCubic](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/nearestOnCubic.md) | The closest point of a cubic to `p`. It takes 33 samples and refines around the best one, so it can pick the wrong part of a curve that passes almost equally close twice. |
| [nearestOnPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/nearestOnPath.md) | The closest point of the whole path to `p`, or null for a path without segments. |
| [parseSvgPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/parseSvgPath.md) | Reads SVG path data into paths, one per subpath. Every command works, absolute and relative: quadratics and arcs become cubics. Anchor types come from the handles. |
| [pathBounds](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/pathBounds.md) | Tight axis-aligned bounds of the path, curves included. |
| [pathFromPoints](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/pathFromPoints.md) | A path through `points` with straight segments and corner anchors. |
| [pathToSvg](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/pathToSvg.md) | The path as SVG path data (`M`, `L`, `C`, `Z`), rounded to `precision` decimals. |
| [removeAnchors](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/removeAnchors.md) | Removes the anchors at `indices`. A closed path left with fewer than two anchors opens. |
| [reversePath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/reversePath.md) | The same path traced the other way: anchors in reverse order, in and out handles swapped. |
| [segmentAt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/segmentAt.md) | Segment `i` as a cubic, from anchor `i` to the next one. A straight segment has its controls on its ends. |
| [segmentCount](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/segmentCount.md) | Number of segments: one fewer than the anchors, or as many when closed. |
| [setAnchorType](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/setAnchorType.md) | Changes anchor `index` to `type`. To `corner` the handles are kept as they are (pass `straighten` to drop them). To `smooth` or `mirrored` they line up, and an anchor without handles gets them from its neighbours. |
| [setHandle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/setHandle.md) | Sets one handle of anchor `index` (an offset from the anchor, or null to remove it). The other handle follows the anchor's type: a `mirrored` twin turns opposite with the same length, a `smooth` one turns opposite and keeps its length. With `breakSymmetry` the anchor becomes a `corner` and the twin stays where it is. |
| [splitCubic](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/splitCubic.md) | Splits a cubic at `t` with de Casteljau's construction. The two halves trace the same curve exactly. |
| [toggleAnchorType](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/path/functions/toggleAnchorType.md) | Switches anchor `index` between a sharp corner (handles removed) and a smooth curve. |

## `pixi-silk/selection`

Selection store and bindOutline, bindGizmo, bindMeasure.

```ts
import { Selection, bindGizmo, bindMeasure, bindOutline } from 'pixi-silk/selection';
```

#### Classes

| [SelectionState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/selection/interfaces/SelectionState.md) | The signals of a [Selection](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/selection/classes/Selection.md). |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [bindGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/selection/functions/bindGizmo.md) | Attaches `gizmo` to the primary item. Returns the function that stops following. |
| [bindMeasure](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/selection/functions/bindMeasure.md) | Shows the primary item's size and the distances to the hovered item with `measure`. Call the returned `sync` again when a condition of `between` changes (a modifier key). The returned `stop` stops following. |
| [bindOutline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/selection/functions/bindOutline.md) | Frames the selection with `outline`. Pass a second outline to show the hovered item too, while it is not selected. Returns the function that stops following. |

## `pixi-silk/signal`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/

signal, computed, watch, batch: the small reactive store every widget state uses.

```ts
import { batch, computed, signal, watch } from 'pixi-silk/signal';
```

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Computed](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/interfaces/Computed.md) | A derived signal. `dispose()` stops it following its sources. |
| [ReadSignal](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/interfaces/ReadSignal.md) | A value you can read and watch. |
| [Signal](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/interfaces/Signal.md) | A value you can read, watch and change. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Listener](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/types/Listener.md) | Called with the new and the previous value. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [batch](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/functions/batch.md) | Runs `fn` and delivers the change notifications once, after it returns, even if it throws. A signal set several times notifies once, with the value from before the batch as `previous`. It notifies nothing if it ends equal to that value. Batches nest. |
| [computed](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/functions/computed.md) | Derives a signal from others: `fn` runs now and again whenever one of the `sources` changes. Only the listed sources are followed. Call `dispose()` when you no longer need it. |
| [signal](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/functions/signal.md) | Creates a signal. `equals` decides when a write is a change (default `Object.is`), so mutating an object in place and setting it again notifies nobody. Set a new object instead. |
| [watch](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/signal/functions/watch.md) | Calls `fn` now and on every change of `source`. Returns the unsubscribe function. |

## `pixi-silk/text`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/

SilkText: distance-field text with outline, shadow and gradient fill. SdfAtlas, layoutText, caret helpers.

```ts
import { SdfAtlas, SilkText, SilkTextGeometry, caretAtPoint } from 'pixi-silk/text';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [SdfAtlas](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SdfAtlas.md) | Glyphs of any CSS font, rasterised with Canvas2D and turned into signed distance fields, in one 8-bit texture every [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md) shares. |
| [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md) | Text from `pixi-silk/text`, drawn from signed distance fields. Glyphs come from any CSS font. They are baked once at one size into a shared [SdfAtlas](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SdfAtlas.md) and drawn scaled, with the same per-pixel anti-aliasing as SilkGraphics. Every glyph is an instance of one mesh. |
| [SilkTextGeometry](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkTextGeometry.md) | Geometry behind every [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md): one unit quad plus one instance of 12 floats per glyph. SilkText owns and fills it. You rarely need it directly. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [CaretHit](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/CaretHit.md) | Where a point lands in a [TextLayout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/TextLayout.md): the nearest caret position. |
| [CaretRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/CaretRect.md) | A caret position in layout units: a vertical segment from `top` to `bottom` at `x`. |
| [FontMetrics](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/FontMetrics.md) | Vertical metrics of a font at the atlas size, measured the way PixiJS `Text` measures them. |
| [FontSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/FontSpec.md) | The font of a glyph: CSS family list, weight and style. Size does not matter, glyphs are baked at one size. |
| [Glyph](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/Glyph.md) | A glyph baked into the atlas. Distances are in raster pixels, at [SdfAtlas.size](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SdfAtlas.md). |
| [GlyphContext](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/GlyphContext.md) | The slice of a 2D canvas context the atlas needs. Pass your own to [SdfAtlasOptions.context](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/SdfAtlasOptions.md) to bake glyphs somewhere else (a worker canvas, or a fake one in tests). |
| [LayoutLine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/LayoutLine.md) | One line of a laid-out text. |
| [LayoutOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/LayoutOptions.md) | What [layoutText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/layoutText.md) needs besides the text. Sizes are in local units. |
| [PlacedGlyph](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/PlacedGlyph.md) | A character placed by [layoutText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/layoutText.md). |
| [SdfAtlasOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/SdfAtlasOptions.md) | Options of an [SdfAtlas](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SdfAtlas.md). |
| [SilkTextOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/SilkTextOptions.md) | Options of a [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md): the style plus the text, anchor and atlas. |
| [SilkTextStyle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/SilkTextStyle.md) | The look of a [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md). Every field can change later through its setter or [SilkText.set](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md). |
| [TextLayout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/TextLayout.md) | Result of [layoutText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/layoutText.md). |
| [TextMeasurer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/TextMeasurer.md) | Widths of characters at the size being laid out, in local units. |
| [TextOutline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/TextOutline.md) | A line drawn around the glyphs, outside their edge. |
| [TextRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/TextRect.md) | A rectangle in layout units, as [rangeRects](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/rangeRects.md) returns them. |
| [TextShadow](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/TextShadow.md) | A soft copy of the glyphs under them: a drop shadow with an offset, a glow without one. |
| [WordSegment](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/WordSegment.md) | A run of text with a kind, as [wordSegments](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/wordSegments.md) returns them. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ResolvedTextStyle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/types/ResolvedTextStyle.md) | A [SilkTextStyle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/interfaces/SilkTextStyle.md) with every field set, as [SilkText.defaults](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md) holds it. |
| [TextAlign](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/types/TextAlign.md) | Horizontal alignment of the lines of a text block. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [caretAtPoint](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/caretAtPoint.md) | The caret position nearest to a point, in the layout's own units (the top left of the text box is 0, 0). Points above the first line or below the last snap to them. `isStop` filters the offsets a caret may take, for example grapheme cluster boundaries. |
| [caretRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/caretRect.md) | Where to draw a caret at `index`, in the layout's units. |
| [caretX](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/caretX.md) | The x of a caret at `index` on line `li`: the nearest caret stop at or before it. |
| [fontString](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/fontString.md) | pixi-silk/text: [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md), text drawn from a shared signed distance field atlas, plus the layout and caret helpers it uses. Not part of the root `pixi-silk` import. |
| [graphemeStops](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/graphemeStops.md) | Offsets where a caret can sit: 0, every grapheme cluster boundary, and the text's length. |
| [isMac](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/isMac.md) | True on macOS, iOS and iPadOS, where Option moves by word and Command by line. |
| [layoutText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/layoutText.md) | Lays out a block of text. Lines break at `\n` and, with `wordWrap`, at spaces. Each character gets its advance, kerning and letter spacing, then the lines are aligned. Widths come from `measure`, so the same layout works for any renderer. |
| [lineOfIndex](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/lineOfIndex.md) | The line a caret at `index` is on. At a wrap, `upstream` picks the end of the first line over the start of the next. |
| [nextWordStop](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/nextWordStop.md) | Where a word jump to the right lands. `mac` (the default on Apple platforms) stops at the end of the next word, like Option + Right. Otherwise it stops at the start of the word after, like Ctrl + Right. |
| [paragraphAt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/paragraphAt.md) | The paragraph around `index`: from the newline before it to the newline after, newlines left out. |
| [previousWordStop](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/previousWordStop.md) | Where a word jump to the left lands: the start of the word before `index`. |
| [rangeRects](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/rangeRects.md) | Highlight boxes of the range `from` to `to`, one per line, the full height of each line box. A range that runs on past a newline gets a sliver of `newline` width at that line's end, so selected empty lines still show. Default newline width a quarter of the font height. |
| [sdfFromAlpha](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/sdfFromAlpha.md) | Turns an anti-aliased coverage bitmap into an 8-bit signed distance field. |
| [silkLabel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/silkLabel.md) | A label factory that makes [SilkText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/classes/SilkText.md) labels, for widgets such as `ValuePill`. The widget's style comes first, then `overrides` (any SilkText option but the text). From the Pixi style it keeps the font, letter spacing, line height, left, center or right alignment, and a plain colour fill. Gradients, strokes and drop shadows of the Pixi style are dropped. |
| [wordAt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/wordAt.md) | The segment (word, run of spaces or punctuation) that holds the character at `index`. |
| [wordSegments](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/text/functions/wordSegments.md) | Splits text into words and the runs between them. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/arc`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/

Angle maths for ring widgets: angleDelta, wrapAngle, snapTo, rubberBand.

```ts
import { AngleAccumulator, TAU, angleDelta, clamp } from 'pixi-silk/ui/arc';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [AngleAccumulator](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/classes/AngleAccumulator.md) | Follows a pointer around a centre and counts full turns, so dragging past the seam keeps adding up instead of jumping back by a turn. `total` is the angle travelled since `reset`. |

#### Variables

| Variable | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [TAU](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/variables/TAU.md) | A full turn in radians, `2 * Math.PI`. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [angleDelta](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/functions/angleDelta.md) | Signed shortest turn from `b` to `a`, in -PI..PI radians. Inputs can be any size. |
| [clamp](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/functions/clamp.md) | Clamps `v` to `min..max`. Expects `min <= max`. |
| [rubberBand](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/functions/rubberBand.md) | Soft limit: inside `min..max` the value passes through, past a limit it keeps moving but slows down and never goes further than `limit` beyond it (the rubber band of scroll views). A `limit` of 0 or less is a hard clamp. |
| [snapTo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/functions/snapTo.md) | Rounds `value` to the nearest `origin + k * step`. A `step` of 0 or less returns `value` unchanged. |
| [wrapAngle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/functions/wrapAngle.md) | Wraps an angle of any size into 0..TAU radians. |
