## `pixi-silk/ui/camera`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/

Camera: pan and zoom a world container, ZoomControl.

```ts
import { Camera, ZoomControl, zoomLabel } from 'pixi-silk/ui/camera';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md) | Pans and zooms a world container over an unbounded canvas. It writes the world's `position` and `scale`, so do not set them yourself while a camera owns the world. |
| [ZoomControl](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/ZoomControl.md) | A `- 100% +` pill for a [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md), 128x32 pixels. The buttons zoom around the view's centre and the percentage fits the content (or resets). The readout follows the camera's zoom signal. Place it in screen space, beside the world. Destroying it unsubscribes from the camera. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [CameraInsets](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/interfaces/CameraInsets.md) | Space to keep free on each side of the view, in CSS pixels. A side left out is 0. |
| [CameraOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/interfaces/CameraOptions.md) | Options of [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md). |
| [CameraState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/interfaces/CameraState.md) | The reactive view of a [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md): where it is now, animations included. |
| [WorldRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/interfaces/WorldRect.md) | A rectangle in world units (the local space of [CameraOptions.world](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/interfaces/CameraOptions.md)). |
| [ZoomControlOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/interfaces/ZoomControlOptions.md) | Options of [ZoomControl](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/ZoomControl.md). |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [zoomLabel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/functions/zoomLabel.md) | Formats a zoom (1 is 100%) as a short percentage. Below 10% it keeps one decimal (`2.5%`), from 10,000% it switches to `k` (`2.5k%`) and from 1,000,000% to `M` (`1.2M%`). |

## `pixi-silk/ui/color`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/

Colour pickers: wheels, rings, OKLCH, channel sliders.

```ts
import { ChannelSliders, ColorModel, ColorPicker, ColorSlider } from 'pixi-silk/ui/color';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ChannelSliders](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ChannelSliders.md) | A stack of [ColorSlider](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorSlider.md)s for one colour model, with optional letter labels. |
| [ColorModel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorModel.md) | The colour every picker edits. Stored as HSV + alpha (so hue survives greys and black), readable and writable as hex, RGB and OKLCH. Bind several pickers to one model and they stay in sync. |
| [ColorPicker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorPicker.md) | Base of the pickers. It binds to a [ColorModel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorModel.md) and turns presses into edits of one part at a time: a drag keeps editing that part even outside it. Handles animate towards the model. The colour field redraws only when it changes, the handles every frame they move. A drag writes to the model continuously and calls `model.commit()` on release. |
| [ColorSlider](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorSlider.md) | A gradient track for one channel. The track shows what each position would give with the rest of the colour unchanged, so moving the hue recolours the saturation track and so on. |
| [ColorWheel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorWheel.md) | Colour wheel: hue around the disc, saturation from the centre out, and a value strip beside it. Origin at the top-left corner, the disc first, then the strip. |
| [Eyedropper](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/Eyedropper.md) | Pick a colour from the scene. `start()` turns the canvas into a sampling surface: a loupe follows the pointer showing the colour under it, a click or tap picks it, Escape or a right click cancels. Add it to the stage first. `start()` moves it to the top of its parent, and while active it takes every pointer press on the canvas. |
| [HueSquarePicker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/HueSquarePicker.md) | Hue ring with a saturation (left to right) / value (bottom to top) square inside. |
| [HueTrianglePicker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/HueTrianglePicker.md) | Hue ring with a saturation / value triangle inside (Blender, Krita, GIMP). The triangle turns so its pure-hue corner points at the hue on the ring. Corners: pure hue, white, black. |
| [OklchPicker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/OklchPicker.md) | Perceptual picker: lightness (top to bottom) against chroma (left to right) for one OKLCH hue, with an OKLCH hue strip below. The area outside sRGB is greyed, with its edge drawn, so colours stay picked by how they look, not by how a screen mixes them. |
| [SwatchPalette](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/SwatchPalette.md) | Swatches to click: a fixed palette or the recent colours of a model, as a grid or a ring. Dragging across swatches previews them, releasing commits. The swatch matching the current colour wears a ring. A ring layout centres on the origin, a grid starts at it. |
| [SwatchPopup](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/SwatchPopup.md) | A ring of swatches that pops up around a point, for quick colour changes mid-stroke. Press, drag towards a colour and release, or tap to keep it open and tap a colour. The centre shows the colour under the pointer and cancels. Picks by angle, like the radial menu. It starts closed: add it to the stage, then call [SwatchPopup.openAt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/SwatchPopup.md). |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ChannelSlidersOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/ChannelSlidersOptions.md) | Options of [ChannelSliders](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ChannelSliders.md). |
| [ColorModelOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/ColorModelOptions.md) | Options of [ColorModel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorModel.md). |
| [ColorPickerOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/ColorPickerOptions.md) | Options every picker accepts. |
| [ColorPickerState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/ColorPickerState.md) | Reactive state of a picker. |
| [ColorSliderOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/ColorSliderOptions.md) | Options of [ColorSlider](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorSlider.md). |
| [ColorWheelOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/ColorWheelOptions.md) | Options of [ColorWheel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorWheel.md). |
| [EyedropperOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/EyedropperOptions.md) | Options of [Eyedropper](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/Eyedropper.md). |
| [HSVA](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/HSVA.md) | Hue in degrees (0..360), saturation, value and alpha (0..1). |
| [HueRingOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/HueRingOptions.md) | Options of the hue-ring pickers. |
| [OklchPickerOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/OklchPickerOptions.md) | Options of [OklchPicker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/OklchPicker.md). |
| [SwatchPaletteOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/SwatchPaletteOptions.md) | Options of [SwatchPalette](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/SwatchPalette.md). |
| [SwatchPopupOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/interfaces/SwatchPopupOptions.md) | Options of [SwatchPopup](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/SwatchPopup.md). |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ColorChannel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/types/ColorChannel.md) | Channel a [ColorSlider](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorSlider.md) edits: HSV (`h`, `s`, `v`), RGB (`r`, `g`, `b`), OKLCH (`L`, `C`, `H`) or alpha (`a`). |
| [ColorSampler](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/types/ColorSampler.md) | Reads the colour under a point, in global (canvas) coordinates. Return null where there is nothing to sample. The lib does not read pixels itself: the app knows its scene (a texture, a render target with `renderer.extract`, or plain maths). |
| [OKLCH](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/types/OKLCH.md) | OKLCH: lightness 0..1, chroma (about 0..0.37 inside sRGB), hue in degrees. |
| [RGB](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/types/RGB.md) | Straight sRGB channels, 0..1. |
| [SwatchPointer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/types/SwatchPointer.md) | What [SwatchPopup.openAt](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/SwatchPopup.md) needs from the pointer event that opened it. |

#### Variables

| Variable | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [HUE\_STOPS](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/variables/HUE_STOPS.md) | Pure hues around the wheel, interpolated in sRGB so every angle is an exact HSV hue. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [angleToHue](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/angleToHue.md) | Hue in degrees, 0 to 360, of a screen angle in radians. The inverse of [hueToAngle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/hueToAngle.md). |
| [channelColor](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/channelColor.md) | The colour at position `t` (0..1) of a channel track, given the rest of the current colour. |
| [checker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/checker.md) | Checkerboard behind transparent colours, clipped to a rounded rectangle: the corner cells carry the corner radius, so it works for pills and swatches without a mask. Needs `radius <= cell`. |
| [drawHandle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/drawHandle.md) | A picker handle: the colour it points at inside a white ring, with a soft shadow and a thin dark rim so it reads on light and dark colours alike. `alpha` below 1 shows a checker inside. |
| [drawSvSquare](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/drawSvSquare.md) | Exact HSV saturation / value square: hue, white fading to the right, black rising from the bottom. Adds three filled rounded rectangles to `g`. `hue` is in degrees. |
| [hexToRgb](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/hexToRgb.md) | 0xRRGGBB to sRGB channels 0..1. |
| [hsvToRgb](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/hsvToRgb.md) | HSV (hue in degrees, saturation and value 0..1) to straight sRGB 0..1. |
| [hueToAngle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/hueToAngle.md) | Screen angle (radians, 12 o'clock is hue 0, clockwise) of a hue in degrees. |
| [inGamut](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/inGamut.md) | True when the OKLCH colour fits in sRGB, with a tolerance of `eps` per channel. |
| [luminance](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/luminance.md) | Relative luminance (WCAG 2), 0 to 1, from straight sRGB. Use it to pick a readable colour on top. |
| [maxChroma](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/maxChroma.md) | Largest chroma at this lightness and hue that still fits in sRGB, found by bisection. 0 for black and white. |
| [oklchToRgb](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/oklchToRgb.md) | OKLCH to sRGB, reducing chroma (keeping lightness and hue) until it fits. |
| [oklchToRgbUnclamped](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/oklchToRgbUnclamped.md) | OKLCH to sRGB, not clamped: channels outside 0..1 mean the colour is outside sRGB. |
| [pureHue](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/pureHue.md) | The colour of a hue at full saturation and value, as 0xRRGGBB. |
| [readChannel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/readChannel.md) | Reads one channel of the model, as 0..1. Hues map 0..360 degrees to 0..1, OKLCH chroma 0..0.37 to 0..1. |
| [rgbToHex](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/rgbToHex.md) | sRGB channels 0..1 to 0xRRGGBB. Channels are clamped to 0..1 and rounded. |
| [rgbToHsv](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/rgbToHsv.md) | sRGB to HSV. Greys have no hue: `hue` is kept for them, so a picker does not jump back to red. |
| [rgbToOklch](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/rgbToOklch.md) | sRGB to OKLCH. Greys keep `hue`. |
| [writeChannel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/writeChannel.md) | Writes one channel of the model from `t` 0..1 (clamped), with the scales of [readChannel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/functions/readChannel.md). OKLCH writes reduce chroma to fit sRGB. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/dial`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/

JogDial and Scrubber: rotary and drag-to-change number inputs.

```ts
import { JogDial, Scrubber } from 'pixi-silk/ui/dial';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [JogDial](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/classes/JogDial.md) | A jog wheel. Press anywhere on it and drag around the centre: the value follows the angle and keeps adding up over full turns. Drag further out for finer control. A flick keeps it spinning and it slows down by itself. Past `min` or `max` it stretches like a rubber band and springs back. With a `step`, the value clicks through detents and each one pulses on the ring. A tap on the right half steps up, on the left half steps down. The dial is centred on its position. |
| [Scrubber](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/classes/Scrubber.md) | A number field you drag, like the ones in 3D and compositing tools. Drag sideways to scrub the value. Shift scrubs finely, Ctrl or Cmd snaps to the step. A tap on the left or right end steps down or up. With `min` and `max` the field fills like a slider. The top-left corner is at (0, 0). |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [DialState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/interfaces/DialState.md) | The reactive state of a [JogDial](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/classes/JogDial.md) or a [Scrubber](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/classes/Scrubber.md). |
| [DialTheme](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/interfaces/DialTheme.md) | Colours of the dials (any Pixi colour). |
| [JogDialOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/interfaces/JogDialOptions.md) | Options of [JogDial](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/classes/JogDial.md). |
| [ScrubberOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/interfaces/ScrubberOptions.md) | Options of [Scrubber](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/classes/Scrubber.md). |

#### Variables

| Variable | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [DIAL\_THEME](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dial/variables/DIAL_THEME.md) | Dark default theme with a blue fill. Override single colours with the `theme` option. |

#### References

##### rubberBand

Re-exports [rubberBand](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/arc/functions/rubberBand.md)

## `pixi-silk/ui/dock`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dock/

ButtonDock: a row or column of SilkButtons that closes the gap when one hides.

```ts
import { ButtonDock } from 'pixi-silk/ui/dock';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ButtonDock](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/dock/classes/ButtonDock.md) | A row or column of [SilkButton](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/button/classes/SilkButton.md)s that closes the gap when one hides and opens it when it comes back, gliding the others into place. Slot sizes follow each button's animated appearance, so the reflow and the fade are one motion. The dock sets each button's `x` (row) or `y` (column), so do not position them yourself. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/gesture`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture/

trackGesture: tap, hold, flick and drag from one target, with slop per pointer kind.

```ts
import { trackGesture } from 'pixi-silk/ui/gesture';
```

| ------ | ------ |
| [GestureHandlers](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture/interfaces/GestureHandlers.md) | Callbacks of [trackGesture](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture/functions/trackGesture.md). Every one is optional. |
| [GestureOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture/interfaces/GestureOptions.md) | Options of [trackGesture](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture/functions/trackGesture.md). |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [trackGesture](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/gesture/functions/trackGesture.md) | Reads tap, hold, flick and drag from one target, with the slop and timing of the pointer kind (fingers get more room than mice). The drag keeps going when the pointer leaves the target. One pointer at a time: a second finger is ignored until the first lifts. Sets the target's `eventMode` to `static` when it was not interactive. Returns a function that stops tracking. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/grid`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/

CanvasGrid: dots or lines that follow the camera.

```ts
import { CanvasGrid, gridLevels } from 'pixi-silk/ui/grid';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [CanvasGrid](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/classes/CanvasGrid.md) | An unbounded grid behind a [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md)'s world. Levels nest (1, 10, 100 by default) and fade in and out with the zoom. The grid lives in screen space and only draws what shows. All vertical lines of a level are one dashed stroke, and so are the horizontal ones. Dots take one stroke per row. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [CanvasGridOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/interfaces/CanvasGridOptions.md) | Options of [CanvasGrid](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/classes/CanvasGrid.md). In `dots` style, `alpha` defaults to 0.32 and `fade` to `[12, 160]`, so dots read as strong as lines. |
| [CanvasGridState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/interfaces/CanvasGridState.md) | The reactive state of a [CanvasGrid](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/classes/CanvasGrid.md). Write to these signals to change the grid. |
| [GridLevel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/interfaces/GridLevel.md) | One level of a [CanvasGrid](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/classes/CanvasGrid.md), as [gridLevels](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/functions/gridLevels.md) returns it. |
| [GridLevelOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/interfaces/GridLevelOptions.md) | Options of [gridLevels](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/functions/gridLevels.md). |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [GridStyle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/types/GridStyle.md) | How a [CanvasGrid](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/classes/CanvasGrid.md) draws: thin `lines` or `dots` at the crossings. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [gridLevels](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/grid/functions/gridLevels.md) | The grid levels to draw at `zoom`, finest first. Each level is `ratio` times the one below. A level fades in as its spacing grows from `fade[0]` to `fade[1]` pixels. Levels nest, so a line of a coarse level is also drawn by the finer ones: each level's `alpha` is what it adds on top. Levels that would add almost nothing are left out. Returns an empty array for a zoom or unit of 0 or less. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/guides`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/

Guides: draggable guide lines from the rulers.

```ts
import { Guides, snapValue } from 'pixi-silk/ui/guides';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Guides](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/classes/Guides.md) | Guide lines over a [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md)'s world. Drag a guide out of a [Ruler](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/classes/Ruler.md): the top ruler gives a horizontal one, the left a vertical one. Drag a guide to move it, drop it back on the ruler to remove it. A pill shows the position while dragging. [Guides.bindSnap](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/classes/Guides.md) feeds the guides to a snap engine, so objects snap to them. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Guide](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/interfaces/Guide.md) | A guide line. Guide objects are replaced, not mutated, when a guide moves. |
| [GuideDrag](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/interfaces/GuideDrag.md) | A guide being dragged: where it is now, and whether letting go here removes it. |
| [GuidesOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/interfaces/GuidesOptions.md) | Options of [Guides](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/classes/Guides.md). |
| [GuidesState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/interfaces/GuidesState.md) | The reactive state of a [Guides](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/classes/Guides.md) layer. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [GuideSnap](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/types/GuideSnap.md) | Snaps a guide while it is dragged. Gets the raw position and the snap distance, both in world units, and returns where the guide should go. Return `at` unchanged to let rounding apply. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [snapValue](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/guides/functions/snapValue.md) | The value of `candidates` within `threshold` of `v` that is nearest, or `v` when none is. A tie keeps the first. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/halo`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo/

ContextHalo: a ring of actions around a selected object.

```ts
import { ContextHalo } from 'pixi-silk/ui/halo';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ContextHalo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo/classes/ContextHalo.md) | Action buttons orbiting the selected object. It follows the target every frame from its bounds, so it tracks drags, tweens and camera moves. The buttons sit on an arc hugging the target's box, on the preferred side. When that side would leave `bounds` the arc turns to the side with the most room. Buttons pop in with a stagger and pop out on `detach()`. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |

| [ContextHaloOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo/interfaces/ContextHaloOptions.md) | Options of [ContextHalo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo/classes/ContextHalo.md). Lengths are in the halo's local units. |

| [HaloItem](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo/interfaces/HaloItem.md) | One action of a [ContextHalo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/halo/classes/ContextHalo.md). |

## `pixi-silk/ui/inspector`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/

Inspector: property fields bound to the selection.

```ts
import { ChoiceField, ColorField, Field, Inspector } from 'pixi-silk/ui/inspector';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ChoiceField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ChoiceField.md) | Segmented choices: a thumb slides to the chosen one and stretches on the way, like `SegmentedSwitch`, but it fills its row and shows no thumb for mixed values. |
| [ColorField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ColorField.md) | A colour swatch and its hex code. A press opens the colour picker. |
| [Field](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/Field.md) | A field: a box of a given size that shows values and edits them. It holds no data. It shows what [Field.show](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/Field.md) gives it and reports edits through its options. The `Inspector` builds these. |
| [Inspector](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/Inspector.md) | A property inspector built from a schema. Rows hold number fields you scrub or type, text fields, switches, segmented choices, colour swatches with a picker and vector rows with an aspect lock. Sections fold. It edits whatever its [InspectorBinding](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/InspectorBinding.md) points at: one object, a multi-selection (differing values show as Mixed) or a set of signals. |
| [NumberField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/NumberField.md) | A number you drag or type. Drag sideways to scrub: Shift is ten times faster, Alt ten times finer. Click without dragging to type a value. Mixed values scrub together, each from its own start. |
| [TextField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/TextField.md) | A one-line text field, edited in place with a [TextEditor](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/textedit/classes/TextEditor.md). |
| [ToggleField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ToggleField.md) | An on and off switch with a springy knob. Mixed values park the knob in the middle. |
| [VectorField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/VectorField.md) | Two number fields in a row, such as X and Y or W and H, with an optional aspect lock. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ChoiceFieldOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ChoiceFieldOptions.md) | Options of [ChoiceField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ChoiceField.md). |
| [ChoiceFieldSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ChoiceFieldSpec.md) | Segmented choices. |
| [ChoiceOption](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ChoiceOption.md) | One choice of a [ChoiceField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ChoiceField.md). |
| [ColorFieldOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ColorFieldOptions.md) | Options of [ColorField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ColorField.md). |
| [ColorFieldSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ColorFieldSpec.md) | A colour swatch that opens a colour picker. Values are 0xRRGGBB numbers. |
| [FieldContext](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/FieldContext.md) | What every field needs from the inspector around it. |
| [FieldEdit](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/FieldEdit.md) | The callbacks of a field's edit gesture: one `begin`, any number of `input`, one `end`. |
| [FieldValue](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/FieldValue.md) | What a field shows for a list of values: the shared value, or `mixed` when they differ. |
| [InspectorBinding](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/InspectorBinding.md) | Where an inspector reads and writes values. `read` returns one value per edited object, so a multi-selection shows mixed values. Write your own to edit a store, or use [bindObjects](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/bindObjects.md) and [bindSignals](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/bindSignals.md). |
| [InspectorOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/InspectorOptions.md) | Options of [Inspector](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/Inspector.md). |
| [InspectorState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/InspectorState.md) | The reactive state of an [Inspector](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/Inspector.md). |
| [NumberFieldOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/NumberFieldOptions.md) | Options of [NumberField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/NumberField.md). |
| [NumberFieldSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/NumberFieldSpec.md) | A number you scrub or type. |
| [SectionSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/SectionSpec.md) | A titled group of fields that folds. |
| [TextFieldOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/TextFieldOptions.md) | Options of [TextField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/TextField.md). |
| [TextFieldSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/TextFieldSpec.md) | A one-line text field. |
| [ToggleFieldOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ToggleFieldOptions.md) | Options of [ToggleField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/ToggleField.md). |
| [ToggleFieldSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/ToggleFieldSpec.md) | An on and off switch. |
| [VectorFieldOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/VectorFieldOptions.md) | Options of [VectorField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/classes/VectorField.md). |
| [VectorFieldSpec](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/VectorFieldSpec.md) | Two numbers in one row, such as X and Y, or W and H with an aspect lock. |
| [WritableSignal](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/interfaces/WritableSignal.md) | A signal the inspector can write. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [ColorPickerFactory](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/types/ColorPickerFactory.md) | Something a colour picker popover shows: a Pixi container that edits a [ColorModel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/color/classes/ColorModel.md). |
| [InspectorField](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/types/InspectorField.md) | One entry of an inspector schema. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [bindObjects](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/bindObjects.md) | Edits plain objects or display objects. Keys are dot paths, such as `position.x`, `alpha` or `style.fill`. Pass the selection's items to edit several objects at once. The list is copied. The binding does not watch the objects: call `Inspector.refresh` after changing them elsewhere. |
| [bindSignals](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/bindSignals.md) | Edits signals, one per key: a settings panel for app state. Changes made elsewhere show in the inspector at once. |
| [fieldValue](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/fieldValue.md) | Folds the values of a multi-selection into what one field shows. Numbers within `epsilon` count as equal. |
| [getPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/getPath.md) | The value at a dot path such as `position.x`. Undefined when a step on the way is missing. |
| [setPath](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/inspector/functions/setPath.md) | Sets the value at a dot path such as `position.x`. Missing objects on the way are not created, and the write is skipped. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/keys`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/

Shared keyboard state: isKeyDown, modifier checks, onKey, isTypingTarget.

```ts
import { altDown, isKeyDown, isTypingTarget, modDown } from 'pixi-silk/ui/keys';
```

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [altDown](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/functions/altDown.md) | True while either Alt / Option is down. |
| [isKeyDown](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/functions/isKeyDown.md) | True while the key with this `KeyboardEvent.code` is down (e.g. `ShiftLeft`, `KeyR`, `Space`). |
| [isTypingTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/functions/isTypingTarget.md) | True when a key event comes from an `input`, `textarea`, `select` or content-editable element. The keys then belong to the field and not to the canvas. |
| [modDown](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/functions/modDown.md) | True while either Control or Meta (Command) is down. |
| [onKey](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/functions/onKey.md) | Calls `fn` on every key press and release on `window`. Auto-repeat presses are skipped. When the window loses focus, every held key gets a synthetic release. Returns the unsubscribe function. |
| [shiftDown](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/keys/functions/shiftDown.md) | True while either Shift is down. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/layers`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/

LayerTree: a layer tree over any data through an adapter, with selection and history.

```ts
import { LayerTree, applyDrop, drawLayerIcon, dropTarget } from 'pixi-silk/ui/layers';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [LayerTree](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/classes/LayerTree.md) | A layer list for editors: rows with expand arrows, icons, names, and eye and lock toggles. It shows any tree through a [TreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/TreeAdapter.md). Only the rows in view are drawn, from a pool. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [DropTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/DropTarget.md) | Where a drop lands. |
| [DropTargetOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/DropTargetOptions.md) | Options of [dropTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/dropTarget.md). |
| [FlatRow](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/FlatRow.md) | One visible row of a flattened tree. |
| [LayerTreeOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/LayerTreeOptions.md) | Options of [LayerTree](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/classes/LayerTree.md). |
| [LayerTreeState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/LayerTreeState.md) | The reactive state of a [LayerTree](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/classes/LayerTree.md). |
| [MoveStep](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/MoveStep.md) | One move of [applyDrop](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/applyDrop.md): where a node was and where it went, as `move()` indices. |
| [PixiTreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/PixiTreeAdapter.md) | What [pixiTreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/pixiTreeAdapter.md) returns: an adapter for containers, plus the lock state it keeps. |
| [PixiTreeOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/PixiTreeOptions.md) | Options of [pixiTreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/pixiTreeAdapter.md). |
| [TreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/TreeAdapter.md) | How a [LayerTree](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/classes/LayerTree.md) reads and changes your data. Only `getChildren` and `getName` are required. The tree never holds its own copy of the data, so it can show a Pixi scene graph, a document model or a state store. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [LayerIcon](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/types/LayerIcon.md) | The icon of a layer row: a built-in kind, or a function that draws one centred on (0, 0). |
| [LayerKind](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/types/LayerKind.md) | What a layer is, for the icon in its row. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [applyDrop](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/applyDrop.md) | Moves `nodes` to a drop target through `adapter.move`, keeping their order, and returns the moves. Undo them with [undoMoves](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/undoMoves.md). Nodes already in place are moved too, so the list replays the same way. |
| [drawLayerIcon](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/drawLayerIcon.md) | Draws the built-in icon of a layer kind. Centred on (x, y) in a `size` box. It adds to `g` and does not clear it. |
| [dropTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/dropTarget.md) | Where a drag over the rows drops, for a pointer at `y` (in row space, 0 at the top of the first row). The middle of a row that takes children drops inside it. The top and bottom halves drop between rows. Below the last row of a subtree, `x` picks how far out the drop goes. Returns null for a drop the tree must refuse, such as a node into itself. |
| [findParent](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/findParent.md) | The parent of `node`, searched from `root`. For adapters without `getParent`. |
| [flattenTree](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/flattenTree.md) | Lists the visible rows of a tree, depth first: the children of `root` (or `root` itself with `showRoot`), and the children of every expanded node under them. |
| [pixiTreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/pixiTreeAdapter.md) | A [TreeAdapter](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/interfaces/TreeAdapter.md) for a Pixi scene graph. Rows list a container's children front to back, so the last child is the top row. Names are `label`. Hiding sets `visible`. Locking turns pointer events off for the node and everything under it. Moves keep each node where it is on screen. |
| [redoMoves](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/redoMoves.md) | Applies the moves of [applyDrop](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/applyDrop.md) again, after [undoMoves](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/undoMoves.md). |
| [topLevelNodes](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/topLevelNodes.md) | The nodes of `nodes` that have no ancestor in `nodes`, in row order. Dragging a group moves its children with it. |
| [undoMoves](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/undoMoves.md) | Puts back what [applyDrop](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/applyDrop.md) moved. |
| [visibleRange](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layers/functions/visibleRange.md) | The rows a list of `count` rows of `rowHeight` shows when scrolled to `scrollY`, plus `overscan` either side. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/layout`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/

SmartContainer and FlexContainer: place and scale a design box in the window, flex children inside it.

```ts
import { FlexContainer, SimulatedViewport, SmartContainer, DEVICES } from 'pixi-silk/ui/layout';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [FlexContainer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/FlexContainer.md) | A container that lays out its children with flexbox. Give a child a style with [FlexContainer.setLayout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/FlexContainer.md) (or add it with [FlexContainer.add](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/FlexContainer.md)). The container places it in its slot once per frame, and only after something changed: a child added, removed or restyled, a new size, or [FlexContainer.invalidate](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/FlexContainer.md). |
| [SimulatedViewport](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/SimulatedViewport.md) | Previews a window size inside a desktop page: a device of that size, scaled down to fit an area (letterboxed), with a bezel and a mask. Add SmartContainers to [SimulatedViewport.content](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/SimulatedViewport.md) and pass the simulator itself as their `viewport`: it is a signal of the device's window, so they lay out as they would on that device. |
| [SmartContainer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/SmartContainer.md) | A responsive container for portrait and landscape screens. It places and scales itself in the window from layout records against a reference resolution. A record has a design box, a reference resolution it fits or covers, an alignment, and optional stretch and corrections. It picks `portraitData` when the window is taller than wide, `landscapeData` otherwise, or the first matching named breakpoint. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Breakpoint](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/Breakpoint.md) | A named layout record that applies when `when` returns true for the window size. |
| [FlexContainerOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/FlexContainerOptions.md) | Options of [FlexContainer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/FlexContainer.md). |
| [Insets](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/Insets.md) | Distances from the window edges, in screen pixels: notches, rounded corners, home bars. |
| [LayoutData](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/LayoutData.md) | Where a SmartContainer sits on screen and how it scales, for one breakpoint. The box is in design units, and the reference resolution sets how design units map to the window. |
| [LayoutDebug](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/LayoutDebug.md) | The handle [layoutDebug](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/layoutDebug.md) returns. |
| [LayoutDebugOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/LayoutDebugOptions.md) | Options of [layoutDebug](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/layoutDebug.md). |
| [MediaRule](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/MediaRule.md) | A style that applies when the root's layout area, or the root SmartContainer's breakpoint, matches. |
| [Placement](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/Placement.md) | Where [placeBox](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/placeBox.md) put a design box: position and scale in the window's space, and the box size. |
| [SimulatedViewportOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/SimulatedViewportOptions.md) | Options of [SimulatedViewport](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/SimulatedViewport.md). |
| [SmartContainerOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/SmartContainerOptions.md) | Options of [SmartContainer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/SmartContainer.md). |
| [SmartLayout](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/SmartLayout.md) | A responsive style: base values, then `portrait` or `landscape`, then each matching `media` rule in order. Rules test the root container's layout area. |
| [SmartStyle](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/SmartStyle.md) | A flex style plus how a child sits in its slot. |
| [ViewportRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/interfaces/ViewportRect.md) | A window rectangle in screen pixels. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [FitMode](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/types/FitMode.md) | How a child scales into its slot, as CSS `object-fit`. `none` keeps its size, `contain` fits inside, `cover` fills and overflows, `scale-down` is `contain` that never enlarges, `fill` stretches both axes. |
| [ViewportSource](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/types/ViewportSource.md) | What a SmartContainer follows: an Application or a renderer (it updates on their `resize`), a signal of a rectangle (it updates on change), or a plain rectangle (call `updateContainer()`). |

#### Variables

| Variable | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [DEVICES](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/variables/DEVICES.md) | Window sizes to preview, in CSS pixels. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [fitScale](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/fitScale.md) | The [x, y] scale of a child of natural size `w` by `h` in a slot, for a fit mode. [1, 1] for `none` or an empty child. |
| [layoutDebug](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/layoutDebug.md) | A debug overlay for SmartContainers and FlexContainers, off by default. It draws over the whole stage, in screen pixels: |
| [placeBox](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/placeBox.md) | Where a record puts its box in a window: the fit scale of the window to the reference resolution, then stretch per axis, then the corrections, then the alignment. Pure, so you can check a layout record without a renderer. Writes into `out` when given, else returns a new object. |
| [simulateViewport](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/simulateViewport.md) | Creates a [SimulatedViewport](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/classes/SimulatedViewport.md). Same as `new SimulatedViewport(options)`. |
| [wrapText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/layout/functions/wrapText.md) | A `measure` that wraps a text child to the width offered: Pixi `Text` (through its style) or `SilkText`. Long copy then breaks into lines instead of overflowing its slot. It turns on the child's `wordWrap` and sets `wordWrapWidth`. Other children are measured by their bounds. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/marquee`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/

Marquee: drag a box to select.

```ts
import { Marquee, combineSelection, quadInside, quadIntersects } from 'pixi-silk/ui/marquee';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Marquee](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/classes/Marquee.md) | Rubber-band selection: drag on empty canvas to pick every object the rectangle touches (or fully contains). Shift adds to the selection, Alt takes away, Escape cancels. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [MarqueeOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/interfaces/MarqueeOptions.md) | Options of [Marquee](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/classes/Marquee.md). |
| [MarqueeState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/interfaces/MarqueeState.md) | The reactive state of a [Marquee](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/classes/Marquee.md). |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [MarqueeMode](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/types/MarqueeMode.md) | `intersect` picks what the rectangle touches, `contain` only what is fully inside. |
| [MarqueeOp](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/types/MarqueeOp.md) | How a drag combines with the selection: replace it, add to it (Shift) or take away from it (Alt). |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [combineSelection](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/functions/combineSelection.md) | Combines a selection with what a marquee covers. `add` appends hits not in `base`, keeping order. `subtract` removes them. Always returns a new array. |
| [quadInside](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/functions/quadInside.md) | True when all four corners of a quad are inside an axis-aligned rectangle, edges included. |
| [quadIntersects](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/marquee/functions/quadIntersects.md) | True when a parallelogram quad (corners in order, as [boundsQuad](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/outline/functions/boundsQuad.md) returns) touches an axis-aligned rectangle. Edges that only touch count. Uses separating axes, so other quads may be wrong. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/measure`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/

Measure: size labels and red lines between objects.

```ts
import { Measure, redlines } from 'pixi-silk/ui/measure';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Measure](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/classes/Measure.md) | Distances and sizes for editors, drawn on an overlay. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [MeasureOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/interfaces/MeasureOptions.md) | Options of [Measure](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/classes/Measure.md). |
| [MeasureRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/interfaces/MeasureRect.md) | An axis-aligned box in the measured space ([MeasureOptions.space](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/interfaces/MeasureOptions.md)). |
| [Redline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/interfaces/Redline.md) | One redline, in the measured space. |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [MeasureTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/types/MeasureTarget.md) | What [Measure](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/classes/Measure.md) can measure: a box in the measured space, or a container. A container is read as the axis-aligned box around its transformed local bounds, every frame. A destroyed or detached container measures as nothing. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [redlines](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/measure/functions/redlines.md) | The redlines between two boxes given as edges (`x0 < x1`, `y0 < y1`). Separate boxes get the horizontal and vertical gaps, with a dashed guide when they do not overlap on the other axis. Overlapping or nested boxes get the distances between matching edges, zero-length ones left out. Pure: it allocates a new array and draws nothing. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/minimap`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/minimap/


#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Minimap](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/minimap/classes/Minimap.md) | A minimap of a [Camera](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/camera/classes/Camera.md)'s world: the content in small, and the view as a frame. Drag the frame to pan, click anywhere to jump there, and scroll to zoom. The map follows the camera on every frame it moves, and glides when the content or the view grows past it. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/odometer`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/

Odometer: a rolling number counter with prefix, decimals and count-up timing.

```ts
import { Odometer, countDuration, digitCount, odometerColumns } from 'pixi-silk/ui/odometer';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Odometer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/classes/Odometer.md) | A number that rolls its digits into place like a mechanical counter, for live readouts, scores and totals. Each column shows two glyphs at most and the frame only moves them, so it stays cheap while counting. Glyphs come from a bitmap font made from the text style on first use. A mask clips the columns. The number is centred on the origin. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [OdometerOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/interfaces/OdometerOptions.md) | Options of [Odometer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/classes/Odometer.md). |
| [OdometerState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/interfaces/OdometerState.md) | The reactive state of an [Odometer](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/classes/Odometer.md). |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [countDuration](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/functions/countDuration.md) | Count-up time in seconds for a jump of `delta` units. With `speedUp` it scales `base` by 0.6 to 3, growing with the log of the jump. Without it, returns `base`. |
| [digitCount](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/functions/digitCount.md) | Number of digit columns the whole part of `units` needs, at least `min`. The sign is ignored. |
| [odometerColumns](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/odometer/functions/odometerColumns.md) | Roll position of every digit column for a value, lowest place first. `units` is the value in its smallest unit (value times 10 ** decimals). Each position runs from 0 to 10. A column only rolls while the column below it passes from 9 to 0, so carries ripple like a mechanical counter. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/panel`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/

Panel: a docked, scrolling side panel drawn on the canvas.

```ts
import { Panel, ScrollView, dockAt, dockRect } from 'pixi-silk/ui/panel';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [Panel](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/classes/Panel.md) | A panel drawn on the canvas, with no DOM: a title bar, a body that scrolls, edges to resize and a chevron that folds it. Drag the title bar to move it, and drop it near a screen edge to dock it there. Size, position and folding glide on springs. Text is SilkText. |
| [ScrollView](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/classes/ScrollView.md) | A clipped area that scrolls its [ScrollView.content](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/classes/ScrollView.md) up and down, the way a native list does. Wheels and trackpads glide to their target, fingers fling with momentum and stretch past the ends, and a slim scrollbar fades in while it moves. |

| [PanelTheme](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/interfaces/PanelTheme.md) | Colours and type of the editor panels. Every panel, the layer tree, the inspector and the minimap read the same tokens, so one theme restyles them all. |
| [PanelViewport](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/interfaces/PanelViewport.md) | The screen a panel docks in: numbers, or signals such as `camera.state.width` and `height`. |

| [dockRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/functions/dockRect.md) | Where a panel of `size` sits when docked to `dock` in a screen of `viewport`. Left and right panels span the height between the insets, top and bottom ones the width. |
| [fitText](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/panel/functions/fitText.md) | Sets `label` to `text`, cut with three dots to fit `width`. Returns true when it was cut. Long names in narrow rows stay on one line. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/pathgizmo`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/

PathGizmo and PenTool: edit and draw Bezier paths.

```ts
import { PathGizmo, PenTool } from 'pixi-silk/ui/pathgizmo';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [PathGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PathGizmo.md) | Edits a cubic bezier path: anchors, handles and segments, plus a pen tool that adds anchors. |
| [PenTool](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PenTool.md) | The pen tool's state machine, without pointer events or drawing: feed it presses, moves and releases in path coordinates and read the path back. A click adds a corner. A drag pulls out mirrored handles, and Alt moves only the outgoing one. A click on the first anchor closes the path, and a click on the last one drops its outgoing handle. [PathGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PathGizmo.md) drives one. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [PathGizmoOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/interfaces/PathGizmoOptions.md) | Options of [PathGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PathGizmo.md). |
| [PathGizmoState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/interfaces/PathGizmoState.md) | The signals of a [PathGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PathGizmo.md). |
| [PathGizmoTheme](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/interfaces/PathGizmoTheme.md) | Colours of a [PathGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PathGizmo.md) (any Pixi colour). |
| [PathSnapInfo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/interfaces/PathSnapInfo.md) | What is being snapped, as the `snap` option gets it. |
| [PenInput](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/interfaces/PenInput.md) | What the pointer is on and which modifiers are down, for [PenTool](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PenTool.md). |
| [PenToolOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/interfaces/PenToolOptions.md) | Options of [PenTool](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PenTool.md). |

#### Type Aliases

| Type Alias | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [PathHit](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/types/PathHit.md) | A part of the path under the pointer. `index` is an anchor index. A segment hit has the curve parameter `t` (0 to 1) and the nearest point (`x`, `y`) in path coordinates. |
| [PathTool](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/types/PathTool.md) | The two modes of a [PathGizmo](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/classes/PathGizmo.md). |
| [PenPhase](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/types/PenPhase.md) | Where the pen tool is. - `idle`: not drawing. The next click starts or continues the path. - `drawing`: the next click adds an anchor. - `dragging`: the button is down on the newly placed anchor, and the drag pulls out its handles. - `closing`: the click closed the path, and the drag shapes the first anchor. |

#### Variables

| Variable | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [PATH\_GIZMO\_THEME](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/pathgizmo/variables/PATH_GIZMO_THEME.md) | Default theme: a blue outline with white anchors. Override single colours with the `theme` option. |

| ------ | ------ |
| [RulerSide](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/types/RulerSide.md) | Which bar of a [Ruler](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/classes/Ruler.md) a point is on. `corner` is the square where both bars meet. |
| [RulerTarget](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/types/RulerTarget.md) | What a [Ruler](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/classes/Ruler.md) highlights: objects, followed every frame through their axis-aligned bounds, or a fixed rectangle in world units. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [niceStep](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/functions/niceStep.md) | The nice step (1, 2 or 5 times a power of ten) at or above `min`. Returns 1 for 0, negative or infinite input. |
| [tickDivisions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/functions/tickDivisions.md) | Minor ticks per label step from [niceStep](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/ruler/functions/niceStep.md): 4 when the step is 2 times a power of ten, else 5. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/snap`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/

SnapEngine and SnapGuides: snap boxes to edges, centres and lines.

```ts
import { SnapEngine, SnapGuides } from 'pixi-silk/ui/snap';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [SnapEngine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapEngine.md) | Pure snapping maths, no drawing. It moves a rectangle so its edges or centre line up with other rectangles, guide lines and points, or so it sits at an equal gap from its neighbours. Each axis picks the nearest candidate within the threshold. Works in whatever space the rectangles are given in, axis-aligned only. Change the properties at any time. |
| [SnapGuides](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapGuides.md) | Draws what a [SnapEngine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapEngine.md) found: dashed alignment lines with end marks, and equal-gap markers with their distance in a pill. Each alignment fades in when found and out when lost, so the guides never slide from one alignment to the next. Add it to the same space as the rectangles you snap. It ignores pointer events. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [SnapEngineOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/interfaces/SnapEngineOptions.md) | Options of [SnapEngine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapEngine.md). Each one sets the engine property of the same name. |
| [SnapGuide](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/interfaces/SnapGuide.md) | A line the snap engine found, ready to draw, in the snapping space. |
| [SnapGuidesOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/interfaces/SnapGuidesOptions.md) | Options of [SnapGuides](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapGuides.md). |
| [SnapRect](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/interfaces/SnapRect.md) | Axis-aligned rectangle in the snapping space. Targets are compared by identity in `exclude`. |
| [SnapResult](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/interfaces/SnapResult.md) | What [SnapEngine.snap](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapEngine.md) found. Add `dx` and `dy` to the moving rectangle to snap it. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/spacing`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/

SpacingMarkers and findSpacing: equal-gap hints.

```ts
import { SpacingMarkers, findSpacing, gapsBetween, spacingFromSnap } from 'pixi-silk/ui/spacing';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [SpacingMarkers](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/classes/SpacingMarkers.md) | Draws smart spacing: gap segments with end bars and a value pill, and thin alignment lines with crosses at their ends. Each marker fades in when found and out when lost, and grows from its middle. Markers are matched across calls by position, rounded to half a unit. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [AlignLine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/interfaces/AlignLine.md) | An alignment line. All values are in the boxes' space. |
| [FindSpacingOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/interfaces/FindSpacingOptions.md) | Options of [findSpacing](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/functions/findSpacing.md). |
| [GapMarker](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/interfaces/GapMarker.md) | A gap marker between two boxes. All values are in the boxes' space. |
| [SpacingMarkersOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/interfaces/SpacingMarkersOptions.md) | Options of [SpacingMarkers](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/classes/SpacingMarkers.md). |
| [SpacingResult](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/interfaces/SpacingResult.md) | What [findSpacing](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/functions/findSpacing.md) found: equal gaps to show and edges or centres that line up. |

#### Functions

| Function | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [findSpacing](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/functions/findSpacing.md) | Smart spacing for a box being dragged among others. When the gap to a neighbour equals another gap in the same row or column, every one of those gaps is returned. Edges and centres that line up with other boxes come back as lines spanning them. Edges only match edges, centres only centres. |
| [gapsBetween](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/functions/gapsBetween.md) | The gaps between consecutive rectangles along an axis, sorted by position, for rows and columns. Overlapping or touching neighbours have no gap. Use it to show the spacing of an auto-layout container's children. It does not check that the rectangles share a row. |
| [spacingFromSnap](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/functions/spacingFromSnap.md) | Converts a [SnapEngine](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/snap/classes/SnapEngine.md) result, or its guides, to a [SpacingResult](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/interfaces/SpacingResult.md) for [SpacingMarkers](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/spacing/classes/SpacingMarkers.md). Spacing guides become gaps, every other guide becomes a line. |

## `pixi-silk/ui/timeline`

URL: https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/

MiniTimeline: frames, keys, loop range and playback.

```ts
import { MiniTimeline } from 'pixi-silk/ui/timeline';
```

#### Classes

| Class | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [MiniTimeline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/classes/MiniTimeline.md) | A compact animation timeline: a ruler you scrub, key dots you select and drag, a loop range and a playhead. Scrubbing past either end stretches like a rubber band. Keys snap to frames. Shift adds to the selection, and a drag on empty track draws a selection box. The wheel zooms around the pointer (sideways or with Shift it pans). A double tap on the ruler zooms in or back out. The top-left corner is at (0, 0). Keys are points in time: there are no curves or values. |

#### Interfaces

| Interface | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [MiniTimelineOptions](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/interfaces/MiniTimelineOptions.md) | Options of [MiniTimeline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/classes/MiniTimeline.md). |
| [TimelineKey](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/interfaces/TimelineKey.md) | One key on a [MiniTimeline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/classes/MiniTimeline.md). |
| [TimelineState](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/interfaces/TimelineState.md) | The reactive state of a [MiniTimeline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/classes/MiniTimeline.md). |
| [TimelineTheme](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/interfaces/TimelineTheme.md) | Colours of a [MiniTimeline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/classes/MiniTimeline.md) (any Pixi colour). |

#### Variables

| Variable | Description |
| ------ | ------ |
| [TIMELINE\_THEME](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/variables/TIMELINE_THEME.md) | Dark default theme of [MiniTimeline](https://pixi-silk.schmooky.dev/api/ui/timeline/classes/MiniTimeline.md). Override single colours with the `theme` option. |

---

# FAQ

## Why is PixiJS Text blurry when I zoom in?

PixiJS `Text` is a bitmap, so scaling it stretches its pixels. `SilkText` from `pixi-silk/text` draws glyphs from a distance field atlas and keeps its edges under a zooming camera.

## Which scripts does SilkText support?

Latin, Greek and Cyrillic UI text. There is no shaping, so right-to-left text, ligatures and combining marks do not work, and emoji come out as one-colour silhouettes. Use PixiJS `Text` for those.

## Do IME and mobile keyboards work in the text editor?

`TextEditor` types through a hidden `<textarea>` that follows the caret, so input methods, mobile keyboards, autocorrect and the clipboard work. Spell check underlines do not show.

## Do pointer events work on SilkGraphics?

Yes. Set `eventMode = 'static'` and listen as usual. Hit tests use the real shapes, so a stroked circle is hollow and a line reacts only within its stroke width.

## Why does my SmartContainer not move or scale?

It needs a `viewport`: pass the Pixi `Application`, a signal or a rectangle as `viewport` or call `setViewport()`. Without one it has no window to place itself in.

## How close is the layout engine to CSS flexbox?

It follows flexbox for direction, wrap, grow, shrink, gaps and alignment. It has no `order`, `overflow` or borders, and an item's default minimum size is 0, not its content size.

## How do I animate a counter or a balance?

Use `Odometer` from `pixi-silk/ui/odometer`. `set(value)` rolls each digit column to the new value, and `skip()` jumps to the end.

## Is the editor kit stable?

No. The editor kit is experimental in 1.0, and its API may change in a minor release.

## Does the editor kit keep my document?

No. The widgets draw and report. You keep the document, and `pixi-silk/selection` and `pixi-silk/history` hold the selection and the undo stack if you want them.

## Do I need DOM panels over the canvas for a scene editor?

No. `pixi-silk/ui/panel` draws dockable panels on the canvas, and `layers`, `inspector` and `minimap` fill them. They are Pixi containers, so they share the scene, its pointer events and its undo history.
